straipsnis

Injury to, torture of or other inhuman treatment of persons protected by international humanitarian law or violation of the protection of their property
1.	Any person who, during war or armed conflict or aggression, occupation or annexation, ordered, incited or organised inhuman treatment of, or treated inhumanely, persons protected by international humanitarian law: seriously injured or made them ill or tortured them; carried out biological or medical experiments on them, unlawfully removed their organ or tissue for transplantation, unlawfully took their blood or otherwise treated them inhumanely; took them hostage; punished them with criminal penalties without a decision of an independent and impartial court or without guarantees of defence in court; applied collective punishments; raped or sexually abused persons, forced them into sexual slavery or forced them to engage in prostitution; forcibly sterilised or impregnated them; used means of intimidation and terror; unlawfully restricted or deprived them of their liberty; separated children from their parents or guardians; created a threat of death by starvation for them; unlawfully seized, confiscated the property of civilians or carried out large-scale appropriation thereof, not justified by military necessity; insultingly humiliated their dignity; forced them to convert to another religion; desecrated the remains of the dead,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to twelve years.
2.	Any person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using deception shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to fifteen years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

straipsnis. Repealed as of 2011-03-31

Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

straipsnis. Forced use of civilians or prisoners of war in the enemy's armed forces

1.	Any person who, during war, armed conflict, aggression, occupation or annexation, in violation of international humanitarian law, forced civilians or prisoners of war to serve in the armed forces of their enemy, used them as a human shield in a military operation, recruited or enlisted children under the age of eighteen into the armed forces or used them in a military operation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
2.	Any person who recruited or enlisted children under the age of eighteen for military service in armed groups not belonging to the state armed forces or used them in a military operation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to twelve years.

straipsnis. Destruction of protected objects, plunder,

destruction or damage of national treasures
Any person who gave an order, not justified by military necessity, to destroy, or destroyed, historical monuments, cultural, artistic, educational, upbringing, scientific or religious objects protected by international treaties or domestic legal acts of the state, used such objects or their surrounding environment for military operations, plundered or appropriated national treasures in occupied or annexed territory or destroyed or damaged them by acts of vandalism and caused significant damage,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to twelve years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

straipsnis. Delay in repatriating prisoners of war

Any person who, after the conclusion of a peace treaty or the cessation of hostilities, unjustifiably delayed the release or repatriation of prisoners of war,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by a fine.

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version as of 2024-01-01

Article

Delay in releasing interned civilians or obstruction of repatriation of other civilians
A person who, after the cessation of hostilities, unjustifiably delayed the release of interned civilians or prevented other civilians wishing to repatriate to their homeland from the territory of armed conflict,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by a fine.

Article

Unlawful use of the emblem, sign or name of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal, United Nations Organization or other emblem (sign) or name protected by international humanitarian law
A person who, during an armed conflict, unlawfully used the emblem of the Red Cross, Red Crescent, Red Crystal, United Nations Organization or other emblem (sign) or name protected by international humanitarian law,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by a fine.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1433, 2008-01-18, Žin., 2008, No. 15-516 (2008-02-05)
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

Article. Aggression

A person who initiated aggression against another state or led it,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.

Article. Prohibited war attack

1.	A person who ordered or carried out a war attack prohibited by international humanitarian law against civilians, medical or civil defence personnel, a military or civilian hospital, a medical station, a vehicle transporting wounded persons or patients, personnel of the International Committee of the Red Cross or a national Red Cross or Red Crescent society, a war

attack against an undefended locality or demilitarized zone, a war attack against a protected cultural property, a war attack without selecting a specific target and knowing that it may result in the death of civilians or the destruction of a civilian object, or a war attack against combatants or other persons who have clearly withdrawn from combat and are not resisting,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.
2.	A person who ordered or carried out a war attack contrary to international humanitarian law against an object posing a great danger to the environment and people – a nuclear power plant, a dam, a storage facility for toxic substances or other similar object, knowing that it may result in very serious consequences, or a war attack using a weapon of mass destruction,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

Article. Use of prohibited means of warfare

A person who, in violation of the provisions of international treaties of the Republic of Lithuania or universally recognised international customs concerning means of warfare or methods of warfare, ordered the use or used prohibited means of warfare or methods of warfare in military operations,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.

Article. Marauding

A person who ordered the plundering or plundered property from killed or wounded persons on the battlefield, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)
Article 1131. Negligent or improper performance of commander's duties
1.	A person who negligently performed the duties of a commander and as a result persons legally or factually subordinate to him committed a criminal act provided for in this Chapter,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to eight years.
2.	A person who performed the duties of a commander knowing that persons legally or factually subordinate to him had committed or intended to commit a criminal act provided for in this Chapter, or disregarding this information and failing to take all necessary and reasonable measures within his power to prevent the commission of these criminal acts or attempts to commit them, or failing to transmit this information to competent institutions for investigation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
3.	A person shall be held liable under paragraph 1 of this Article only in cases where the act provided for therein was committed through negligence.
The Code has been supplemented with an article:
No. XI-1291, 2011-03-22, Žin., 2011, No. 38-1805 (2011-03-31)

i. k. 2023-13986

CHAPTER XVI
CRIMES AGAINST THE INDEPENDENCE, TERRITORIAL INTEGRITY AND CONSTITUTIONAL ORDER OF THE LITHUANIAN STATE

Article. Coup d'état

1.	A person who organised or participated in a conspiracy to carry out a coup d'état or participated in
a coup,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to twenty years.
2.	A person who, while committing the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, used armed force or if his act resulted in serious consequences,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.

3.	A person who participated in a conspiracy provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if he voluntarily provided a state institution with important information about a coup d'état being prepared.

Article. Attempt on the life of the President of the Republic of Lithuania

A person who attempted on the life of the President of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.

Article

Attempt on the life of a representative of another state or international public organisation
A person who attempted on the life of a representative of another state or international public organisation officially present in the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment.

Article. Treason

A citizen of the Republic of Lithuania who, during wartime or after the declaration of a state of war, went over to the enemy's side or assisted the enemy in acting against the Lithuanian state,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.

Article. Assisting another state in acting against the Republic of Lithuania

1.	A person who assisted another state or its organisation in acting against the Republic of Lithuania – its
constitutional order, sovereignty, territorial integrity, defence or economic capacity, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to seven years.
2.	A person who committed a criminal act provided for in this Article shall be released from criminal liability if, before being recognised as a suspect, he confessed to having committed the criminal act and actively cooperated in identifying representatives of a foreign state or its organisation and their activities directed against the constitutional order, sovereignty, territorial integrity, defence or economic capacity of the Republic of Lithuania.
3.	Paragraph 2 of this Article shall not apply to a person who has already been released from criminal liability on the grounds established in this Article or in Article 119 of this Code, nor if the commission of the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article resulted in the death of a person or other serious consequences.
4.	A legal person shall also be liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1117, 2022-05-24, published in TAR 2022-06-01,
i. k. 2022-11875

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Espionage

1.	A person who, with the purpose of transmitting to a foreign state or its organisation, seized, purchased or otherwise collected information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania, or transmitted this information to a foreign state, its organisation or their representative,
shall be punished by imprisonment from four to ten years.
2.	A person who, carrying out a task of another state or its organisation, seized, purchased or otherwise collected or transmitted information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania, or other information of interest to foreign intelligence,
shall be punished by imprisonment from six to fifteen years.
3.	A person who has committed the criminal act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if he confessed to having committed the criminal act before being recognised as a suspect and actively cooperated in identifying the representatives of the foreign state or its organisation to whom the collected information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania was to be or was transmitted.
4.	A person who has committed the criminal act provided for in paragraph 2 of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if he confessed to having committed the

criminal act before being recognised as a suspect and actively cooperated in identifying the representatives of the foreign state or its organisation and in clarifying their activities related to the collection or transmission of information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania or other information of interest to foreign intelligence.
5.	Paragraphs 3 and 4 of this Article shall not apply to a person who has already been released from criminal liability on the grounds established in this Article or in Article 118 of this Code, and also if the commission of the act provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article resulted in the death of a person or other serious consequences.
6.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1117, 2022-05-24, published TAR 2022-06-01,
i. k. 2022-11875

Article. Collaboration

A citizen of the Republic of Lithuania who, under conditions of occupation or annexation, assisted unlawful authority structures in consolidating the occupation or annexation, suppressing the resistance of the population of Lithuania or otherwise aided the unlawful authority in acting against the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Article. Creation and activities of anti-constitutional groups or organisations

A person who created organisations or armed groups having the purpose of unlawfully changing the constitutional order of the Lithuanian state, attempting against its independence, violating the integrity of the territory, or participated in the activities of such organisations or groups,
shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.

Article. Public incitement to violate the sovereignty of the Republic of Lithuania by violence

A person who publicly incited to violate the sovereignty of the Republic of Lithuania by violence – to change its constitutional order, to overthrow the lawful authority, to attempt against its independence or to violate the integrity of the territory, to create armed groups for these purposes or to commit other crimes provided for in this Chapter which attempt against the Lithuanian state,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Article. Abuse of official powers

A person who, being authorised to represent the Republic of Lithuania in maintaining relations with another state or an organisation of another state or in an international public organisation, exceeded the powers or deliberately failed to perform duties, or performed them improperly and thereby committed an act contrary to the interests of the Republic of Lithuania, as a result of which substantial damage was or could have been caused,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.

Article 1231. Violation of international sanctions or restrictive measures established in the laws of the Republic of Lithuania
1.	A person who violated international sanctions implemented in the Republic of Lithuania or restrictive measures established in the laws of the Republic of Lithuania and thereby caused substantial damage to the interests of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A legal person shall also be liable for the act provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an article:
No. IX-2169, 2004-04-27, Žin., 2004, No. 72-2492 (2004-04-30)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1866, 2023-03-30, published TAR 2023-04-05,
i. k. 2023-06473

Article. Unlawful handling of information which is a state secret

A person who unlawfully acquired or transferred information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania, or unlawfully kept material objects the content of which or information about which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania, if there were no elements of espionage,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Article. Disclosure of a state secret

1.	A person who disclosed information which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania, if that information was entrusted to him or he learned it due to his service, work or performance of public functions, but there were no elements of espionage,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	The act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article is a crime also in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Loss of a state secret

1.	A person who destroyed, damaged or lost a document, object or other material object entrusted to him due to service, work or performance of public functions, the content of which or information about which is a state secret of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	The act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article is a crime also in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Desecration of state symbols

A person who publicly tore down, tore up, broke, destroyed, defiled or otherwise desecrated the flag or coat of arms of the Lithuanian state or publicly mocked the anthem of the Lithuanian state,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article

Desecration of symbols of a foreign state, the European Union or an international public organisation
A person who tore down, tore up, broke, destroyed, defiled or otherwise desecrated an officially displayed coat of arms or flag of a foreign state, the flag of the European Union or an international public organisation,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)

CHAPTER XVII
CRIMES AGAINST HUMAN LIFE

straipsnis. Murder

1.	A person who murdered another person,
shall be punished by imprisonment from seven to fifteen years.
2.	A person who murdered:
1)	a minor;
2)	a person in a helpless condition;
3)	his close relative or family member;
4)	a pregnant woman;
5)	two or more persons;
6)	by torture or in another particularly cruel manner;
7)	in a manner dangerous to the life of other persons;
8)	for hooligan motives;
9)	for mercenary motives;

10)	for the victim's service or performance of a citizen's duties;
11)	seeking to conceal another crime;
12)	seeking to obtain the victim's organ, tissue or cells;
13)	seeking to express hatred towards a group of persons or a person belonging to it on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, religion, beliefs or views.
Straipsnio punkto pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1065, 2022-04-28, paskelbta TAR 2022-05-04,
i. k. 2022-09434

shall be punished by imprisonment from eight to twenty years or by imprisonment for life.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, Nr. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
Nr. X-1527, 2008-05-08, Žin., 2008, Nr. 59-2200 (2008-05-24)
Nr. X-1597, 2008-06-12, Žin., 2008, Nr. 73-2796 (2008-06-27)
Nr. XI-303, 2009-06-16, Žin., 2009, Nr. 77-3168 (2009-06-30)

straipsnis. Murder in a state of strong agitation

A person who murdered a person suddenly in a state of strong agitation due to an unlawful or extremely insulting act of the victim towards him or a person close to him,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six years.

straipsnis. Murder of a newborn

A mother who murdered her newborn due to a condition caused by childbirth shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for up to five years.

straipsnis. Negligent deprivation of life

1.	A person who through negligence deprived another person of life shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for up to four years.
2.	A person who through negligence deprived two or more persons of life shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six years.
3.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article by violating special safety rules of conduct established by legal acts,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to eight years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for an act provided for in paragraph 3 of this Article.

straipsnis. Incitement to suicide or driving to suicide

A person who incited a person to commit suicide or by cruel or deceitful conduct drove a person to suicide,
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or by arrest, or by imprisonment for up to four years.

straipsnis. Assistance in suicide

A person who, at the request of a hopelessly ill person, assisted him in committing suicide,
shall be punished by community service or by arrest, or by imprisonment for up to four years.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, Nr. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

XVIII	SKYRIUS
CRIMES AGAINST HUMAN HEALTH

straipsnis. Serious health impairment

1.	A person who injured or caused illness to a person, if as a result the victim lost sight, hearing, speech, fertility, pregnancy or was otherwise seriously maimed, contracted a serious incurable or long-lasting illness posing a real threat to life or severely disrupting a person's psyche, or lost a large part of professional or general working capacity, or the victim's body was irreparably disfigured,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to ten years.

2.	A person who seriously injured or caused serious illness to:
1)	a minor;
2)	a person in a helpless condition;
3)	his close relative or family member;
4)	a pregnant woman;
5)	two or more persons;
6)	by torture or in another particularly cruel manner;
7)	in a manner dangerous to the life of other persons;
8)	for hooligan motives;
9)	for mercenary motives;
10)	for the victim's service or performance of a citizen's duties;
11)	seeking to conceal another crime;
12)	seeking to obtain the victim's organ, tissue or cells;
13)	seeking to express hatred towards a group of persons or a person belonging to it on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, religion, beliefs or views.
Straipsnio punkto pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1065, 2022-04-28, paskelbta TAR 2022-05-04,
i. k. 2022-09434

shall be punished by imprisonment from two to twelve years.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. X-1527, 2008-05-08, Žin., 2008, Nr. 59-2200 (2008-05-24)
Nr. X-1597, 2008-06-12, Žin., 2008, Nr. 73-2796 (2008-06-27)
Nr. XI-303, 2009-06-16, Žin., 2009, Nr. 77-3168 (2009-06-30)

straipsnis. Serious health impairment in a state of strong agitation

A person who seriously injured a person suddenly in a state of strong agitation due to an unlawful or extremely insulting act of the victim towards him or a person close to him,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for up to four years.

straipsnis. Serious health impairment through negligence

1.	A person who through negligence seriously injured or caused serious illness to a person shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for up to three years.
2.	A person who through negligence seriously injured or caused serious illness to two or more persons shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.
3.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article by violating special safety rules of conduct established by legal acts,
shall be punished by imprisonment for up to seven years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for an act provided for in paragraph 3 of this Article.

straipsnis. Non-serious health impairment

1.	A person who injured or caused illness to a person, if as a result the victim lost a small part of professional or general working capacity or was ill for a long time, but did not suffer the consequences specified in paragraph 1 of Article 135 of this Code,
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or by arrest, or by imprisonment for up to three years.
2.	A person who caused non-serious injury or illness to:
1)	a minor;
2)	a person in a helpless condition;
3)	his close relative or family member;
4)	a pregnant woman;
5)	two or more persons;
6)	by torture or in another particularly cruel manner;
7)	in a manner dangerous to the life of other persons;
8)	for hooligan motives;
9)	for mercenary motives;
10)	for the victim's service or performance of a citizen's duties;

11)	seeking to conceal another crime;
12)	seeking to obtain the victim's organ, tissue or cells;
13)	seeking to express hatred towards a group of persons or a person belonging to it on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, religion, beliefs or views.
Straipsnio punkto pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1065, 2022-04-28, paskelbta TAR 2022-05-04,
i. k. 2022-09434

shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. X-1527, 2008-05-08, Žin., 2008, Nr. 59-2200 (2008-05-24)
Nr. X-1597, 2008-06-12, Žin., 2008, Nr. 73-2796 (2008-06-27)
Nr. XI-303, 2009-06-16, Žin., 2009, Nr. 77-3168 (2009-06-30)

straipsnis. Non-serious health impairment through negligence

1.	A person who through negligence injured or caused illness to a person, if as a result the victim lost a small part of professional or general working capacity or was ill for a long time, but did not suffer the consequences specified in paragraph 1 of Article 135 of this Code,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for up to one year.
2.	A person who through negligence caused non-serious injury or illness to two or more persons shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for up to one year.
3.	A person shall be held liable for an act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his legal representative, or a demand by the prosecutor.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. IX-1706, 2003-07-04, Žin., 2003, Nr. 74-3423 (2003-07-25)

Article. Infliction of physical pain or minor health impairment

1.	A person who by beating or otherwise using violence caused physical pain to a person or slightly injured or temporarily made him ill,
shall be punished by community service or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2.	A person who committed the act specified in paragraph 1 of this Article against his close relative or family member,
shall be punished by community service or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A person who committed the act specified in paragraph 1 of this Article against a minor or by torturing the victim,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XII-1649, 2015-04-23, published TAR 2015-04-30,
i. k. 2015-06588

4.	A person shall be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or an application by his legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor, or if a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of domestic violence.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XII-1649, 2015-04-23, published TAR 2015-04-30,
i. k. 2015-06588

Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1706, 2003-07-04, Žin., 2003, No. 74-3423 (2003-07-25), correction: Žin., 2003, No. 76
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)

Article. Explanation of concepts

1.	The signs of health impairments provided for in Articles 135, 138 and 140 of this Chapter are described by the rules for determining the extent of health impairment approved by the Government of the Republic of Lithuania or an institution authorised by it.

2.	In this Code, family members of a person who has committed a criminal act are parents (adoptive parents), children (adopted children), brothers, sisters and their spouses or partners living together with him, as well as the spouse of the person who committed the criminal act or a person with whom the person who committed the criminal act lives together without having registered a marriage (partnership), parents (adoptive parents), children (adopted children), brothers, sisters of the spouse or partner and their spouses or partners.
3.	A minor referred to in this Code is a person under fourteen years of age.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

XIX	CHAPTER
CRIMES DANGEROUS TO HUMAN HEALTH AND LIFE

Article. Illegal abortion

1.	A doctor who has the right to perform abortion operations who performed an abortion at the request of a patient, if there were contraindications or if it was performed not in a health care institution,
shall be punished by community service or by restriction of liberty, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A health care specialist who does not have the right to perform abortion operations who performed an abortion at the request of a patient in a health care institution,
shall be punished by community service or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A person who, without having the right to perform abortion operations, terminated a pregnancy at the request of the woman herself,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Coercion to undergo an illegal abortion

A person who by using psychological violence against the victim or her relatives coerced a pregnant woman to undergo an illegal abortion,
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

Article. Abandonment without assistance when there is danger to human life

A person who, having caused danger or having a duty to care for the victim, did not assist him when there was danger to this person's life, although he had the possibility to provide him with assistance,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article

Threat to kill or seriously impair a person's health or terrorisation of a person
1.	A person who threatened to kill a person or seriously impair his health, if there was sufficient ground to believe that the threat could be carried out,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who terrorised a person by threatening to blow up, set fire to or commit another act dangerous to life, health or property, or systematically intimidated a person by using psychological violence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or an application by his legal representative, a request by the prosecutor, or if a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of domestic violence.

4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)

XX	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST HUMAN LIBERTY
Amended title of the chapter:
No. XIV-576, 2021-10-14, published TAR 2021-10-22,
i. k. 2021-22115

Article. Unlawful deprivation of liberty

1.	A person who unlawfully deprived a person of liberty, if there were no signs of abduction of a person as a hostage,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using violence or by creating danger to the life or health of the victim, or by holding the victim in captivity for more than 48 hours,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3.	A person who unlawfully deprived a person of liberty by confining him in a psychiatric hospital not due to
illness,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Article. Trafficking in human beings

1.	A person who sold, bought, otherwise transferred or acquired, recruited, transported or held in captivity a person by using physical violence or threats or otherwise depriving him of the possibility to resist, or by taking advantage of the victim's dependence or vulnerability, or by using deception, or by accepting or paying money, or by receiving or granting other benefit to a person who actually controls the victim, if the perpetrator knew or intended that the victim, regardless of whether he consented, would be exploited under slavery or slavery-like conditions, for prostitution, pornography or other forms of sexual exploitation, for forced or sham marriage, for forced labour or services, including begging, for committing a criminal act or for other purposes of exploitation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XII-2340, 2016-05-12, published TAR 2016-05-24,
i. k. 2016-13906

2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article against two or more victims or by creating danger to the life of the victim, or by participating in an organised group, or knowing or intending that the organ, tissue or cells of the victim would be removed, or being a civil servant or a person performing public administration functions and exercising powers,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to twelve years.
3.	A person who has been a victim of an act provided for in this Article may be released from criminal liability for a criminal act which he was forced to directly commit as a result of the act provided for in this Article committed against him.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XI-2198, 2012-06-30, Žin., 2012, No. 82-4276 (2012-07-13)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Exploitation for forced labour or services

1. A person who, by using physical violence, threats, deception or other means specified in Article 147 of this Code, unlawfully compelled a person to perform certain work or provide certain services, including begging,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by compelling a person to work or provide services under slavery or other inhuman conditions,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3. A person who has suffered from the act provided for in this Article may be released from criminal liability for a criminal act which he was compelled to commit directly as a result of the act provided for in this Article committed against him.
Supplemented by a paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-3350, 2020-11-05, published TAR 2020-11-18,
i. k. 2020-24234

4. A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article."

Article 1472. Use of a person's forced labour or services
1. A person who used a person's labour or services provided, including prostitution, knowing or having and being able to know that the person performs this work or provides these services because physical violence, threats, deception or other means specified in Article 147 of this Code were used against him for the purpose of exploitation,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in this Article shall be released from criminal liability if he voluntarily reported it to a law enforcement institution before being recognised as a suspect and actively cooperated in identifying a person who suffered from trafficking in human beings (Article 147) or purchase or sale of a child (Article 157) and in detecting any of these criminal acts.
3. A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented by an Article:
No. XI-2198, 2012-06-30, Žin., 2012, No. 82-4276 (2012-07-13)

Article. Restriction of a person's freedom of action

1. A person who demanded that a person perform unlawful acts or refrain from performing lawful acts, or otherwise act according to the perpetrator's instruction by using mental coercion against the victim or his relatives,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person shall be liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only in the case where there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his legal representative, a request of the prosecutor or where a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of violence in a close environment.
3. A legal person shall also be liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)
Article 1481. Unlawful stalking of a person
1. A person who, against the clearly expressed will of a person and without lawful grounds, systematically stalked this person, if as a result the victim was forced to change his place of residence or work, or educational institution or other negative impact was made on his social life or negative impact was made on his emotional state, shall commit a misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
2. A person shall be liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only in the case where there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his legal representative, a request of the prosecutor or where a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of violence in a close environment.
Supplemented by an Article:
No. XIV-576, 2021-10-14, published TAR 2021-10-22,
i. k. 2021-22115

CHAPTER XXI
CRIMES AND MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST A PERSON'S FREEDOM OF SEXUAL SELF-DETERMINATION AND INVIOLABILITY

Article. Rape

1. A person who had sexual intercourse with a person against his will by using physical violence or threatening to use it immediately, or otherwise depriving him of the ability to resist, or by taking advantage of the helpless state of the victim,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2. A person who raped a person with a group of accomplices shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.
3. A person who raped a minor,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
4. A person who raped a child,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.
5. A person shall be liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only in the case where there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his legal representative, a request of the prosecutor or where a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of violence in a close environment.
6. A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 3 and 4 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)

Article. Sexual assault

1. A person who satisfied sexual passion with a person against his will by anal, oral or other physical contact by using physical violence or threatening to use it immediately, or otherwise depriving him of the ability to resist, or by taking advantage of the helpless state of the victim,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2. A person who performed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article with a group of accomplices shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3. A person who performed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article against a minor shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years.
4. A person who performed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article against a child shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to thirteen years.
5. A person shall be liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only in the case where there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his legal representative, a request of the prosecutor or where a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of violence in a close environment.
6. A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 3 and 4 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Coercion to Sexual Intercourse

1.	A person who, by threatening to use violence, by using other psychological coercion, or by taking advantage of a person's dependence, coerced that person to have sexual intercourse or otherwise satisfy sexual desire with the perpetrator or another person,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article against a minor shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3.	A person shall be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his or her legal representative, a request by the prosecutor, or if a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of domestic violence.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 2 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)

No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)
No. XII-776, 2014-03-13, published in TAR 2014-03-24,
i. k. 2014-03404
Article 151¹. Satisfaction of Sexual Desire by Violating the Freedom of Sexual Self-Determination and (or) Inviolability of a Minor
1.	An adult who had sexual intercourse or otherwise satisfied sexual desire with a person under sixteen years of age, where there were no signs of rape, sexual assault, or coercion to sexual intercourse,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
2.	A person who had sexual intercourse or otherwise satisfied sexual desire with a minor having offered, promised to provide, or provided to him or her or to another person money or other form of remuneration therefor, where there were no signs of rape, sexual assault, or coercion to sexual intercourse,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A father, mother, guardian, caretaker, or other legal representative of the child, or a person having powers provided by law in respect of a minor, or a person who abused trust, authority, or influence over a minor, who had sexual intercourse or otherwise satisfied sexual desire with that minor, where there were no signs of rape, sexual assault, or coercion to sexual intercourse,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
Amendment to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-3350, 2020-11-05, published in TAR 2020-11-18,
i. k. 2020-24234

4.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 3 of this Article with a person under sixteen years of age
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
Supplemented by a paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-3350, 2020-11-05, published in TAR 2020-11-18,
i. k. 2020-24234

Article. Sexual Harassment

1.	A person who, seeking sexual communication or gratification, harassed a person dependent by service or otherwise by vulgar or similar acts, proposals, or hints, shall have committed a misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.
2.	A person shall be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his or her legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.

Article 152¹. Solicitation of a Person Under Sixteen Years of Age

1.	An adult who proposed to a person under sixteen years of age to meet with the aim of having sexual intercourse or otherwise satisfying sexual desire or exploiting him or her for the production of pornographic material, and who, following this proposal, took concrete steps for the meeting to take place,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented by an Article:
No. XII-776, 2014-03-13, published in TAR 2014-03-24,
i. k. 2014-03404

Article. Corruption of a Person Under Sixteen Years of Age

1.	A person who committed acts of corruption of a person under sixteen years of age
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

CHAPTER XXII CRIMES AGAINST THE HONOUR AND DIGNITY OF A PERSON

Amendment to the title of the Chapter:

i. k. 2022-27570

Article. Defamation

1.	A person who disseminated through a public information medium information about another person that does not correspond to reality and that may humiliate or degrade that person or undermine trust in him or her, or defamed a person as having committed an intentional crime,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his or her legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.

CHAPTER XXIII
CRIMES AND MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST A CHILD AND THE FAMILY

Article. Abduction of a Child or Substitution of Newborns

1.	A person who abducted another person's child or substituted newborns shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
2.	A father, mother, or close relative who abducted his or her own or his or her relatives' child from a children's institution or from a person with whom the child lawfully resided
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version as of 2024-01-01

Article. Purchase or sale of a child

1.	A person who offered to purchase, otherwise acquire, sold, purchased, otherwise transferred, acquired, recruited, transported or held in captivity a child knowing or seeking that, regardless of the child's consent, he or she would be unlawfully adopted, exploited under slavery or slavery-like conditions, for prostitution,

pornography, other forms of sexual exploitation, forced or fictitious marriage, forced labour or services, including begging, commission of a criminal act or other purposes of exploitation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between three and twelve years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XII-2340, 2016-05-12, published in TAR 2016-05-24,
i. k. 2016-13906

2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article against two or more children or a minor, or creating danger to the life of the victim, or participating in an organised group, or knowing or seeking that an organ, tissue or cells of the victim would be removed, or being a civil servant or a person performing public administration functions and exercising powers,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between five and fifteen years.
3.	A person who has suffered from an act provided for in this Article may be released from criminal liability for a criminal act which he or she was directly forced to commit as a result of the act provided for in this Article committed against him or her.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XI-2198, 2012-06-30, Žin., 2012, No. 82-4276 (2012-07-13)

Article. Abandonment of a child

A father, mother or guardian or other lawful representative of a child who abandoned a minor child unable to take care of himself or herself without necessary supervision with the intention to get rid of him or her,
shall be punished by community service or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article. Involvement of a child in a criminal act

A person who by persuading, requesting, bribing, threatening, deceiving or in any other way involved a child in a criminal act,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Article

Involvement of a child in the use of medicines or other intoxicating substances or substances specified in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Control of Certain Doping Substances
A person who involved a child in the use of medicines or other intoxicating non-narcotic substances or substances specified in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Control of Certain Doping Substances for non-therapeutic purposes,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-1674, 2015-05-07, published in TAR 2015-05-18,
i. k. 2015-07560

Article. Involvement of a child in drinking

1.	A person who involved a child in drinking,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who intoxicated a child shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.

Article. Exploitation of a child for pornography

1.	A person who recruited, forced or involved a child in participating in pornographic events, or exploited a child for such purposes, or exploited a child for the production of pornographic material, or profited from such activity of a child,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
2.	A person who participated in a pornographic event in which a child was involved shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1992, 2004-01-29, Žin., 2004, No. 25-760 (2004-02-14)
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)
No. XII-776, 2014-03-13, published in TAR 2014-03-24,
i. k. 2014-03404

Article

Abuse of the rights or duties of parents, guardian or caretaker or other lawful representatives of a child
A person who abused the rights or duties of a father, mother, guardian or caretaker or other lawful representatives of a child by physically or mentally oppressing the child, leaving him or her without supervision for a long time or treating the child in a similarly cruel manner,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.

Article. Evasion of maintenance of a child

A person who evaded the obligation under a court decision to maintain a child, pay funds for the maintenance of a child or provide other necessary material support to a child,
shall be punished by community service or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
CHAPTER XXIV
CRIMES AGAINST THE INVIOLABILITY OF A PERSON'S PRIVATE LIFE

Article. Unlawful violation of the inviolability of a person's dwelling

1.	A person who unlawfully, secretly or openly, using deception or violence or in any other way, against the will of the owner or his or her authorised persons, intruded into another person's dwelling house, flat or other residential premises or its appurtenances, including the protected territory of the dwelling,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person shall be held liable for the act provided for in this Article only if there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his or her lawful representative, a request of the prosecutor or if a pre-trial investigation has been initiated upon establishing signs of domestic violence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-501, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3772 (2013-07-13)

Article. Violation of the inviolability of a person's correspondence

1.	A person who unlawfully intercepted a consignment or parcel sent by post or through a courier service provider or unlawfully intercepted, recorded or monitored messages sent by a person through electronic communications networks, or unlawfully recorded, listened to or monitored a person's conversations through electronic communications networks, or otherwise violated the inviolability of a person's correspondence,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Unlawful collection of information about a person's private life

1.	A person who unlawfully collected information about a person's private life,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article

Unlawful disclosure or use of information about a person's private life

1.	A person who, without the consent of a person, publicly disclosed, used or used for the benefit of other persons information about another person's private life, if he or she learned that information due to his or her service or profession or while performing a temporary task, or collected it by committing an act provided for in Articles 165–167 of this Code,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
3.	A person shall be held liable for the act provided for in this Article only if there is a complaint of the victim or a statement of his or her lawful representative, or a request of the prosecutor.
CHAPTER XXV
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST EQUALITY AND FREEDOM OF CONSCIENCE OF A PERSON

Article

Discrimination on the grounds of nationality, race, sex, origin, religion or other group affiliation
A person who has committed acts aimed at preventing a group of people or a person belonging to it, on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, belief, convictions or views, from participating on equal terms with others in political, economic, social, cultural, labour or other activities, or at restricting the rights and freedoms of such a group of people or a person belonging to it,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-343, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08026
No. XIV-1065, 2022-04-28, published TAR 2022-05-04,
i. k. 2022-09434

Article

Incitement against any nation, race, ethnic, religious or other group of people
1.	A person who, with the aim of disseminating, produced, acquired, sent, transported, kept items in which a group of people or a person belonging to it is mocked, denigrated, hatred is incited or discrimination is incited on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, belief, convictions or views, or violence or physical assault against such a group of people or a person belonging to it is incited, or disseminated them,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.
2.	A person who publicly mocked, denigrated, incited hatred or incited discrimination against a group of people or a person belonging to it on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, belief, convictions or views,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A person who publicly incited violence or physical assault against a group of people or a person belonging to it on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, belief, convictions or views, or financed or otherwise materially supported such activity,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-330, 2009-07-09, Žin., 2009, No. 87-3663 (2009-07-23)

Article 1701. Creation and activities of groups and organisations having the aim of discriminating against a group of people or inciting against it

1.	A person who created a group of accomplices or an organised group or an organisation having the aim of discriminating against a group of people on the grounds of age, sex, sexual orientation, disability, race, skin colour, nationality, language, origin, ethnic origin, social status, belief, convictions or views, or inciting against it, or participated in the activities of such a group or organisation, or financed or otherwise materially supported such a group or organisation,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-343, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08026

2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented by Article:
No. XI-330, 2009-07-09, Žin., 2009, No. 87-3663 (2009-07-23)

Article 1702. Public approval of international crimes, crimes of the USSR or Nazi Germany, their denial or gross trivialisation
1.	A person who publicly approved genocide or other crimes against humanity or war crimes recognised by legal acts of the Republic of Lithuania or the European Union or by final judgments of courts of the Republic of Lithuania or international courts, denied or grossly trivialised them, where this was done in a threatening, abusive or insulting manner or public order was or could have been disrupted as a result, and also a person who publicly approved aggression against the Republic of Lithuania committed by the USSR or Nazi Germany, genocide or other crimes against humanity or war crimes committed by the USSR or Nazi Germany, or other very serious or serious crimes against the Republic of Lithuania or very serious crimes against the inhabitants of the Republic of Lithuania committed in 1990–1991 by persons who carried out or participated in aggression against the Republic of Lithuania, denied or grossly trivialised them, where this was done in a threatening, abusive or insulting manner or public order was or could have been disrupted as a result,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented by Article:
No. XI-901, 2010-06-15, Žin., 2010, No. 75-3792 (2010-06-29)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1065, 2022-04-28, published TAR 2022-05-04,
i. k. 2022-09434

Article. Obstruction of the performance of religious rites or religious ceremonies

A person who, by obscene words, insolent acts, threats, mockery or other indecent acts, disrupted the worship or other rites or ceremonies of a religious community or association recognised by the State, shall have committed a misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.

CHAPTER XXVI
CRIMES AGAINST THE ELECTORAL RIGHTS OF PERSONS AND THE ORDER OF ELECTIONS OR REFERENDUMS OF THE PRESIDENT OF THE REPUBLIC OF LITHUANIA, THE SEIMAS, THE EUROPEAN PARLIAMENT, MUNICIPAL COUNCILS AND MAYORS, THE ORDER OF FINANCING OF POLITICAL ORGANISATIONS, ANALYTICAL CENTRES AND POLITICAL CAMPAIGNS
Amendments to the title of the Chapter:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
Amended title of the Chapter:
No. XIII-2563, 2019-11-28, published TAR 2019-12-10,
i. k. 2019-19871
No. XIV-1384, 2022-07-19, published TAR 2022-07-20,
i. k. 2022-15855

Article. Obstruction of the exercise of the right to vote or to participate in a referendum

1.	A person who used mental coercion, deception or bribery against a person having the right to vote or to participate in a referendum, seeking to influence his will in exercising his right to vote or to refrain from it or to participate in a referendum, or committed these acts against a person having the right to be elected,

shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who organised the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, committed them systematically, or where as a result the elections or referendum were declared invalid,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
No. XI-2393, 2012-11-08, Žin., 2012, No. 133-6759 (2012-11-17)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1462, 2018-06-30, published TAR 2018-07-11,
i. k. 2018-11777

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article

Forgery of an election or referendum document or use of a forged election or referendum document
1. A person who, seeking to influence the results of an election or referendum, forged a list of voters, a list of citizens entitled to participate in a referendum, a vote counting protocol, an election or referendum ballot, produced a large quantity of false election or referendum ballots, or used a forged or false election or referendum document, or organised the forgery, production or use of these documents,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, if as a result the election or referendum was declared invalid,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-2393, 2012-11-08, Žin., 2012, No. 133-6759 (2012-11-17)

Article. Incorrect counting of election votes

1. A person who, seeking to influence the results of an election or referendum, incorrectly counted and recorded the votes of voters or citizens who participated in the referendum, or organised incorrect counting or recording of votes,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, if as a result the election or referendum was declared invalid,
shall be punished by arrest, or a fine, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-2393, 2012-11-08, Žin., 2012, No. 133-6759 (2012-11-17)

Article

Destruction, damage, seizure or concealment of an election or referendum document
1. A person who destroyed, damaged, seized or concealed a list of voters or citizens entitled to participate in a referendum, an election or referendum ballot or a vote counting protocol, if as a result the election or referendum was declared invalid or it had a significant influence on the results of the election or referendum, or organised the destruction, damage, seizure or concealment of these documents,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. XI-2393, 2012-11-08, Žin., 2012, No. 133-6759 (2012-11-17)
Article 1751. Illegal financing of political organisations, analytical centres or political campaigns

1. A person who illegally provided funds or other support, the value of which exceeds 500 times the amount of the MGL, to finance a political organisation, analytical centre or political campaign directly or indirectly, or illegally accepted or used these funds or support in the activities of a political organisation, analytical centre or political campaign,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1384, 2022-07-19, published in TAR 2022-07-20,
i. k. 2022-15855

CHAPTER XXVII
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST SOCIAL RIGHTS OF A PERSON

Article. Violation of occupational safety and health requirements

1. An employer or a person authorised by him who violated the occupational safety and health requirements established in the Law on Occupational Safety and Health or other legal acts, if as a result a serious or fatal accident at work occurred or other serious consequences arose,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
2. The act provided for in this Article is criminal only in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-1871, 2015-06-25, published in TAR 2015-07-10,
i. k. 2015-11240
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Obstruction of trade union activities

A person who obstructed the lawful activities of a trade union or its member shall commit a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)
CHAPTER XXVIII
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST PROPERTY, PROPERTY RIGHTS AND PROPERTY INTERESTS

Article. Theft

1. A person who misappropriated another person's property,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who openly misappropriated another person's property, or in a public place misappropriated another person's property from a person's clothing, handbag or other carrier (pickpocketing), or misappropriated another person's property by breaking into a vehicle,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3. A person who misappropriated another person's property by breaking into premises, a communications cable duct system, a storage facility or a protected area, or misappropriated another person's property of large value, or misappropriated property or a part thereof constituting the infrastructure of legal persons of strategic or significant importance to national security,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
4. A person who misappropriated another person's property of very large value, or misappropriated valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance, or misappropriated another person's property while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
5. A person who misappropriated another person's property of small value shall commit a criminal misdemeanour and shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.

6. A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 5 of this Article only if there is
a complaint by the victim or a statement by his legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.
7. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2, 3 and 4 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Illegal use of energy and communications services

1. A person who, by illegally connecting to an energy supply or communications network or storage facility, by distorting meter readings or by other illegal means, used electricity or thermal energy, gas, water, telecommunications or other things having economic value and thereby caused property damage to another person,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article and thereby caused large property damage to another person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XII-1554, 2015-03-19, published in TAR 2015-03-23,
i. k. 2015-04087

3. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article and thereby caused small property damage to another person shall commit a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
4. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
5. A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 3 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.

straipsnis. Robbery

1.	A person who, by using physical violence or threatening to use it immediately or otherwise depriving the victim of the ability to resist, seized another's property,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	A person who committed robbery by breaking into premises or by using a non-firearm weapon or other object capable of injuring a person, or who committed robbery by seizing another's property of large value,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Straipsnio dalies pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

3.	A person who committed robbery by using a firearm or explosive or who committed robbery by seizing another's property of very large value or valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance, or who committed robbery while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years.
Straipsnio dalies pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis. Extortion of Property

1.	A person who, without lawful grounds, openly or covertly, for his own or others' benefit, forced another person to transfer property, grant a proprietary right or release from a proprietary obligation, or to perform other acts of a proprietary nature, or to refrain from them, by threatening the victim or another person

with the use of physical violence, destruction or damage of his property, disclosure of compromising or other information the revelation of which is undesirable, or by using other mental coercion,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	A person who extorted another's property of large value or who, while extorting property, used physical violence, deprived a person of liberty, destroyed or damaged his property or otherwise caused him large proprietary damage,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
Straipsnio dalies pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

3.	A person who extorted another's property of very large value or valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance, or who extorted property while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
Straipsnio dalies pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis. Fraud

1.	A person who, by deception, for his own or others' benefit, acquired another's property or proprietary right, avoided a proprietary obligation or cancelled it,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who, by deception, for his own or others' benefit, acquired another's property or proprietary right of large value, avoided a proprietary obligation of large value or cancelled it,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
3.	A person who, by deception, for his own or others' benefit, acquired another's property or proprietary right of very large value,
or valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance, or avoided a proprietary obligation of very large value or cancelled it, or committed fraud while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A person who, by deception, for his own or others' benefit, acquired another's property or proprietary right of small value, avoided a proprietary obligation of small value or cancelled it, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.
5.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 4 of this Article only where there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.
6.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 of this Article.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

1821 straipsnis. Manipulation of Sports Competitions
1.	A person who unlawfully influenced the fair course or result of professional sports competitions,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if he voluntarily reported it to a law enforcement institution before being recognised as a suspect and actively cooperated in solving this criminal act.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article.

straipsnis. Misappropriation of Property

1.	A person who misappropriated another's property or proprietary right entrusted to him or in his possession,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who misappropriated another's property or proprietary right of large value entrusted to him or in his possession,

shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
3.	A person who misappropriated another's property or proprietary right of very large value entrusted to him or in his possession, or valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A person who misappropriated another's property or proprietary right of small value entrusted to him or in his possession, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.
5.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 of this Article.
6.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 4 of this Article only where there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.
