straipsnis. Embezzlement of Property

1.	A person who embezzled another's property or proprietary right entrusted to him or in his possession,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who embezzled another's property or proprietary right of large value entrusted to him or in his possession,

shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
3.	A person who embezzled another's property or proprietary right of very large value entrusted to him or in his possession, or valuables of great scientific, historical or cultural significance,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.

shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.

Article. Appropriation of Found Property

A person who appropriated a found treasure, items of great scientific, historical or cultural significance, another found item of great value or someone else's property of great value that accidentally came into their possession,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,

Article. Destruction or Damage of Property

1.	A person who destroyed or damaged someone else's property,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

2.	A person who destroyed or damaged someone else's property in a generally dangerous manner or by dismantling or damaging an installation or unit, if people could have been harmed as a result, or destroyed, dismantled or damaged property or a part thereof constituting the infrastructure of legal persons of strategic or significant importance to national security, or destroyed or damaged someone else's property of great value or items of great scientific, historical or cultural significance,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XII-1554, 2015-03-19, published TAR 2015-03-23,
i. k. 2015-04087

3.	A person who destroyed or damaged someone else's property of small value, has committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
4.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 3 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by their legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor. Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. XII-500, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3771 (2013-07-13)

Article. Destruction or Damage of Property by Negligence

1.	A person who by negligence destroyed or damaged someone else's property and caused substantial pecuniary damage to the victim or destroyed or damaged items of great scientific, historical or cultural significance,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by violating special safety rules of conduct established by legal acts,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A person shall be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by their legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 2 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

Article. Acquisition or Disposal of Property Obtained by Criminal Means

1.	A person who acquired, possessed, used or disposed of property knowing that such property was obtained by criminal means or by participating in criminal activity,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four
years.
2.	A person who acquired, possessed, used or disposed of property of great value or items of great scientific,
historical or cultural significance knowing that such property or items were obtained by criminal means or by participating in criminal activity,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six
years.
3.	A person who acquired, used or disposed of property of small value knowing that such property
was obtained by criminal means or by participating in criminal activity, has committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

1891 Article. Illicit Enrichment

1.	A person who owned property with a value exceeding 900 MGL by right of ownership, knowing that such property could not have been acquired by lawful income,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

2.	A person who acquired the property referred to in paragraph 1 of this Article from third parties shall be released from criminal liability for illicit enrichment if they reported this to law enforcement authorities before the service of a notice of suspicion and actively cooperated in establishing the origin of such property.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an Article:
No. XI-1199, 2010-12-02, Žin., 2010, No. 145-7439 (2010-12-11)
Note. Under Article 1891, only persons who possess property meeting the characteristics established in Article 1891 of the Criminal Code after the entry into force of this law (No. XI-1199) (2010-12-11) shall be brought to criminal liability.

Article. Explanation of Concepts

1.	Property referred to in this Chapter is of very great value when its value exceeds the amount of 900 MGL, of great value when its value exceeds the amount of 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL, of small value when its value exceeds the amount of 3 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 10 MGL.
2.	Pecuniary damage referred to in this Chapter is damage exceeding the amount of 10 MGL but not exceeding the amount of 400 MGL. Pecuniary damage referred to in this Chapter is small when its amount exceeds the amount of 3 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 10 MGL, substantial when its amount exceeds the amount of 400 MGL.
3.	Lawful income referred to in Article 1891 of this Chapter is income obtained from activities not prohibited by legal acts, regardless of whether it was included in accounting in the manner established by legal acts or not. Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

XXIX	CHAPTER CRIMES AGAINST INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY

Amended Chapter title:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Appropriation of Authorship

1.	A person who published or publicly disclosed someone else's work (including computer programs) or a part thereof under their own name,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who, by taking advantage of their official position or by using mental coercion, forced the author of a work (including computer programs) or a part thereof to recognise another person as a co-author or successor to the author's rights or to renounce the right of authorship,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-2025, 2023-06-01, published TAR 2023-06-07,
i. k. 2023-11217

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article

Illegal reproduction, making publicly available via computer networks (internet), distribution, transportation or possession of unlawful copies of copyright or related rights objects
1. A person who unlawfully reproduced a copyright or related rights object or a part thereof for commercial purposes or distributed, transported or possessed unlawful copies thereof for commercial purposes, where

the total value of the copies according to the prices of lawful copies, or where there are none, – according to the prices of the originals of the reproduced works exceeded the amount of 400 MGL,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who for commercial purposes unlawfully made publicly available via computer networks (internet) a copyright or related rights object or a part thereof, where as a result property damage was caused which exceeded the amount of 400 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

i. k. 2023-11217

Article. Destruction or alteration of information on copyright or related rights management

1. A person who without the permission of the copyright or related rights subject for commercial purposes destroyed or altered information on copyright or related rights management, where according to that information the work, the author of the work, another copyright subject or performer, performance of the work, phonogram, phonogram producer, another related rights subject is identifiable, as well as information on the conditions and procedure for the use of the work, its performance or phonogram, including all numbers or codes conveying information marked on copies of the work, performance recording or phonogram or provided at the time of their public disclosure,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-2025, 2023-06-01, published TAR 2023-06-07,
i. k. 2023-11217

Article

Unlawful removal of technical protection measures for copyright or related rights
1. A person who unlawfully removed any technical protection measures which copyright or related rights subjects use to implement or protect their rights, or for commercial purposes manufactured, imported, exported, possessed, transported or distributed devices enabling the removal of those technical protection measures (decoders, decoding cards or other devices) or software, passwords, codes or other similar data,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-2025, 2023-06-01, published TAR 2023-06-07,
i. k. 2023-11217

Article. Infringement of industrial property rights

1. A person who infringed the exclusive rights of a patent owner or design owner, where as a result major damage was caused,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

XXX CHAPTER

CRIMES AGAINST THE SECURITY OF ELECTRONIC DATA AND INFORMATION SYSTEMS
Amendments to the title of the Chapter:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Unlawful interference with electronic data

1. A person who unlawfully destroyed, damaged, deleted or altered electronic data or by technical equipment, software or other means restricted the use of such data causing damage,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article with respect to electronic data of multiple information systems or electronic data of an information system of strategic importance to national security or of major importance to state governance, economy or financial system or by using another person's personal data, or causing major damage,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-1777, 2015-06-11, published TAR 2015-06-18,
i. k. 2015-09697
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Unlawful interference with an information system

1. A person who unlawfully disrupted or interrupted the operation of an information system causing damage, shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article with respect to multiple information systems or an information system of strategic importance to national security or of major importance to state governance, economy or financial system or by using another person's personal data, or causing major damage,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Unlawful Interception and Use of Electronic Data

1.	A person who unlawfully monitored, recorded, intercepted, acquired, held, appropriated, disseminated or otherwise used non-public electronic data,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who unlawfully monitored, recorded, intercepted, acquired, held, appropriated, disseminated or otherwise used non-public electronic data of strategic importance to national security or of major importance to state governance, economy or financial system,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
Article 1981. Unlawful Access to an Information System
1.	A person who unlawfully accessed an information system or a part thereof by breaching the security measures of the information system,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who unlawfully accessed an information system or a part thereof of strategic importance to national security or of major importance to state governance, economy or financial system,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

XXXI	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE ECONOMY AND BUSINESS ORDER

Article. Smuggling

1.	A person who, when transporting across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds 150 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 400 MGL, failed to present them for customs control or otherwise evaded such control,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who, when transporting across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL, failed to present them for customs control or otherwise evaded such control,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A person who, when transporting across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds the amount of 900 MGL, failed to present them for customs control or otherwise evaded such control or who, without authorisation, transported movable cultural property or antique objects across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1836, 2018-12-20, published in TAR 2018-12-28,
i. k. 2018-21859
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article 1991. Customs Fraud
1.	A person who imported into the Republic of Lithuania from a Member State of the European Union goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds 150 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 400 MGL, and failed to present them for customs control of the Republic of Lithuania or another Member State of the European Union or otherwise evaded such control,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who imported into the Republic of Lithuania from a Member State of the European Union goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL, and failed to present them for customs control of the Republic of Lithuania or another Member State of the European Union or otherwise evaded such control,

shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A person who imported into the Republic of Lithuania from a Member State of the European Union goods which are required to be presented to customs and the value of which exceeds the amount of 900 MGL, and failed to present them for customs control of the Republic of Lithuania or another Member State of the European Union or otherwise evaded such control,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1836, 2018-12-20, published in TAR 2018-12-28,
i. k. 2018-21859
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article 1992. Unlawful Handling of Excisable Goods
1.	A person who, in breach of the established procedure, acquired, held, transported, sent, used or sold excisable goods the value of which exceeds 150 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 400 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who, in breach of the established procedure, acquired, held, transported, sent, used or sold excisable goods the value of which exceeds 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A person who, in breach of the established procedure, acquired, held, transported, sent, used or sold excisable goods the value of which exceeds the amount of 900 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1836, 2018-12-20, published in TAR 2018-12-28,
i. k. 2018-21859
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Unlawful Failure to Export Goods or Products from the Republic of Lithuania

1.	A person who unlawfully failed to export across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods or products the value of which exceeds 150 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 400 MGL and which, according to transit or export documents, should have been exported from the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who unlawfully failed to export across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods or products the value of which exceeds 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL and which, according to transit or export documents, should have been exported from the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A person who unlawfully failed to export across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania goods or products the value of which exceeds the amount of 900 MGL and which, according to transit or export documents, should have been exported from the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1836, 2018-12-20, published in TAR 2018-12-28,

Article

Illegal production, storage, transportation or sale of homemade strong alcoholic beverages, non-denatured or denatured ethyl alcohol, their dilutions (mixtures)
Article title amended:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

1.	A person who, with the purpose of sale, illegally produced, stored, transported homemade strong alcoholic beverages, non-denatured or denatured ethyl alcohol, their dilutions (mixtures) or sold them,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Article part amendments:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

2.	A person who, with the purpose of sale, illegally produced, stored, transported fifty or more litres of homemade strong alcoholic beverages, non-denatured or denatured ethyl alcohol, their dilutions (mixtures) or sold them,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A person who, without the purpose of sale, illegally produced, stored, transported ten or more litres of homemade strong alcoholic beverages or non-denatured or denatured ethyl alcohol, their dilutions (mixtures), shall commit a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty.
Article amendments:
No. IX-1706, 2003-07-04, Žin., 2003, No. 74-3423 (2003-07-25)
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-2105, 2012-06-21, Žin., 2012, No. 78-4027 (2012-07-04)

Article. Illegal engagement in economic, commercial, financial or professional activity

1.	A person who engaged in economic, commercial, financial or professional activity on a commercial basis or on a large scale without having a licence (permit) for the activity for which it is required, or in another illegal manner, shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who engaged in prohibited economic, commercial, financial or professional activity, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Illegal activity of a legal person

1.	A person who engaged on a large scale in activity not provided for in the founding documents of a public legal person,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2.	A person who established, acquired a legal person used to conceal illegal activity, or managed a legal person used to conceal illegal activity,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Use of another's trademark for goods or services

1.	A person who, without having permission, marked a large quantity of goods or goods of high value with another's trademark for goods, acquired, transported, stored them for commercial purposes or offered them for sale or used another's trademark for services and thereby caused major damage,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Fraudulent statement about the activity of a legal person

1.	A person who, on behalf of a legal person, provided fraudulent data about the activity or property of the legal person in an official report or application and by this statement misled a state or European Union institution, an international public organisation, a creditor, a participant of the legal person or another person, who as a result suffered major pecuniary damage,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Article part amendments:

No. XIII-2334, 2019-07-16, published in TAR 2019-07-26,
i. k. 2019-12378

2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article

Use of credit, loan, targeted support, subsidy or grant not for its intended purpose or established procedure
1.	A person who, having received targeted support, subsidy or grant of a value of 400 MGL or more, used them not for their intended purpose or established procedure,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2.	A person who, having received a credit or loan, used them not for their intended purpose or established procedure and did not repay them on time, and thereby caused major pecuniary damage to the creditor, guarantor or another person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A person who, having received targeted support, subsidy or grant, used them not for their intended purpose or established procedure and thereby caused major pecuniary damage to a state or European Union institution, an international public organisation or another legal or natural person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
4.	A person who, having received targeted support, subsidy or grant, used them not for their intended purpose or established procedure and thereby caused very major pecuniary damage to a state or European Union institution, an international public organisation or another legal or natural person or did so while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
5.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XIII-2334, 2019-07-16, published in TAR 2019-07-26,
i. k. 2019-12378
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Credit fraud

1.	A person who by fraud obtained credit, loan, targeted support, subsidy, grant, letters of guarantee or bank guarantee or other credit obligations,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who by fraud obtained targeted support, subsidy or grant and thereby caused major pecuniary damage to a state or European Union institution, an international public organisation or another legal or natural person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
Article part amendments:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

3.	A person who by fraud obtained targeted support, subsidy or grant and thereby caused very major pecuniary damage to a state or European Union institution, an international public organisation or another legal or natural person or committed fraud while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
Article supplemented with part:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Debtor's Bad Faith

1.	A person who, due to his difficult economic situation or insolvency, when bankruptcy was clearly imminent, not having the possibility to satisfy the claims of all creditors, satisfied the claims of only one or several of them or secured the claims of one or several creditors and thereby caused substantial pecuniary damage to the remaining one or more creditors,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who, due to his difficult economic situation or insolvency, when bankruptcy was clearly imminent, concealed, squandered, transferred, remitted abroad or sold at an unjustifiably low price property that could have been provided to cover debts, and thereby caused substantial pecuniary damage to one or more creditors,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 or 2 of this Article causing very substantial pecuniary damage to one or more creditors,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Criminal Bankruptcy

1.	A person who, by knowingly mismanaging an enterprise, caused its bankruptcy and thereby caused substantial pecuniary damage to one or more creditors,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article causing very substantial pecuniary damage to one or more creditors,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Commercial Espionage

A person who unlawfully acquired information considered a trade secret or transmitted this information to another person,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Disclosure of Trade Secret

A person who disclosed information considered a trade secret, which was entrusted to him or which he learned of due to his service or work, where this act caused substantial pecuniary damage to the injured person,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Explanation of Concepts

1.	Pecuniary damage referred to in this Chapter is substantial when its amount exceeds the sum of 400 MGL but does not exceed the sum of 900 MGL, very substantial when its amount exceeds the sum of 900 MGL.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

2.	The value of items (goods) referred to in Articles 199, 1991, 1992 and 200 of this Chapter is calculated according to their customs value, including mandatory taxes payable.
3.	Homemade strong alcoholic beverages referred to in Article 201 of this Chapter are alcoholic beverages whose volumetric ethyl alcohol concentration exceeds 18 percent.

4.	The scale of illegal engagement in economic, commercial, financial or professional activity and illegal activity of a legal person referred to in Articles 202 and 203 of this Chapter is large when the income received from this activity during the last twelve months or the value of goods not included in accounting in the established manner exceeds the sum of 500 MGL.
Supplemented by a part of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

5.	Legal persons referred to in this Chapter are any legal persons, except the State, municipality, State and municipal institution and establishment, and international public organisation.
Amendment to the numbering of the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,

XXXII	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE FINANCIAL SYSTEM

Article. Production, Possession or

Circulation of Counterfeit Money or Securities
1.	A person who produced counterfeit or forged genuine money or securities of Lithuania or another State in circulation or officially approved but not yet released into circulation, or acquired, imported, exported, transported, possessed or circulated counterfeit or forged money or securities of Lithuania or another State in circulation or officially approved but not yet released into circulation, or produced, acquired, possessed or circulated equipment, software, electronic data or other means, including any security features or special materials, directly intended for or adapted to the production of counterfeit money or securities or the forgery of genuine money or securities,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four
years.
2.	A person who produced, forged, acquired, imported, exported, transported, possessed or circulated
a large quantity or large value of counterfeit or forged money or securities of Lithuania or another State in circulation or officially approved but not yet released into circulation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
3.	A person who, having received as genuine, circulated a small quantity of knowingly counterfeit or forged money or securities of Lithuania or another State in circulation,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
4.	A person who, having the right to produce genuine money, but in violation of the established procedure or conditions for the production of money, produced money of Lithuania or another State in circulation or officially approved but not yet released into circulation, as well as a person who possessed, imported, exported, transported, acquired or circulated such money, knowing that it was produced in violation of the established procedure or conditions for the production of money,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
5.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)
Amendments to the Article:

Article

Manufacturing of counterfeit electronic means of payment, forgery of genuine electronic means of payment or unlawful disposal of electronic means of payment or their data
1. A person who manufactured one or more counterfeit electronic means of payment or parts thereof or forged one or more genuine electronic means of payment or unlawfully acquired, imported, exported, transported, kept, transferred or disposed of one or more other persons' counterfeit or forged electronic means of payment, or unlawfully acquired, imported, exported, transported, kept, transferred or disposed of data of one or more other persons' electronic means of payment or their user authentication means, sufficient to initiate a financial transaction, or manufactured, acquired, imported, exported, transported, kept, transferred or disposed of technical equipment, software or other means directly intended for or adapted to manufacturing counterfeit electronic means of payment or parts thereof or forging genuine electronic means of payment,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-3350, 2020-11-05, published TAR 2020-11-18,
i. k. 2020-24234

2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Unlawful use of electronic means of payment or their data

1. A person who unlawfully initiated or performed one or more financial transactions using one or more other persons' counterfeit or forged electronic means of payment or by unlawfully using data of one or more other persons' electronic means of payment or their user authentication means, or by using knowingly false data of one or more authentication means, or recognised as lawful the knowingly unlawful use of one or more other persons' counterfeit or forged electronic means of payment,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Legalisation of property obtained by criminal means

1. A person who, seeking to conceal or legalise property of his own or of another person or to help another person participating in criminal activity to avoid the legal consequences of that activity, acquired property, possessed it, used it or transferred it to other persons, performed financial transactions related to that property, concluded transactions, used it in economic or commercial activity, otherwise converted it or falsely indicated that it was obtained from lawful activity, knowing that that property was obtained by criminal means or by participating in criminal activity, as well as a person who concealed the true nature, source, location, disposal, movement or ownership or other rights related to property of his own or of another person, knowing that that property was obtained by criminal means or by participating in criminal activity,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-699, 2021-11-23, published TAR 2021-12-03,
i. k. 2021-25127

2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

No. XII-702, 2013-12-19, published TAR 2014-01-07,
i. k. 2014-00068

Article

Trading in financial instruments using publicly undisclosed information

1. A person who, knowing publicly undisclosed information about material events for an issuer or other publicly undisclosed information related to an issuer or its financial instruments, directly or indirectly concluded a transaction in respect of financial instruments of that issuer or gave, amended or withdrew an order to buy or sell financial instruments of that issuer, or transmitted such information to third parties, or recommended, encouraged or proposed to third parties to acquire or transfer financial instruments of the issuer to which the publicly undisclosed information was related or to amend or withdraw an order to buy or sell financial instruments of that issuer, if as a result that person avoided major property damage or obtained major property benefit for himself or other persons, or caused major property damage to the issuer or another market participant, or serious consequences arose for the financial instruments market and (or) the financial system,
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-2554, 2016-06-30, published TAR 2016-07-13,
i. k. 2016-20291

Article. Manipulation of the financial instruments market

1. A person who directly or indirectly concluded a transaction or gave an order to buy or sell financial instruments or disseminated false, misleading or incomplete information about a benchmark or an issuer or its financial instruments, seeking to influence the calculation of a benchmark or artificially increase or decrease the price of financial instruments, or to create a false or misleading impression about the supply, demand or price of financial instruments, or to maintain an unusual or artificial price of financial instruments, or directly or indirectly concluded a transaction or gave an order to buy or sell financial instruments using fictitious means or in another manner prohibited by legal acts, or unlawfully influenced the calculation of a benchmark, if as a result that person avoided major property damage or obtained major property benefit for himself or other persons, or caused major property damage to the issuer or another market participant, or serious consequences arose for the financial instruments market and (or) the financial system,
shall be punished by restriction of liberty or a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-3350, 2020-11-05, published TAR 2020-11-18,
i. k. 2020-24234

2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-2554, 2016-06-30, published TAR 2016-07-13,
i. k. 2016-20291

Article. Non-payment of taxes

1. A person who, having submitted a declaration or a report or other document confirmed in the established manner, being solvent, did not pay in due time taxes calculated according to them exceeding 400 MGL after a state-authorised institution reminded about the obligation to pay taxes,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to four
years.
2. A person who, having committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, did not pay taxes calculated for him exceeding 900 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

straipsnis. Submission of false data on income, profit or property

1.	A person who, seeking to evade taxes the amount of which exceeds 400 MGL, entered knowingly false data on a person's income, profit, property or their use into a declaration or into a report or other document confirmed in the prescribed manner and submitted them to a state-authorised institution,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, where the amount of taxes exceeds 900 MGL or participating in an organised group,

shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis. Failure to submit a declaration, report or other document

1.	A person who, seeking to evade taxes or other contributions of more than 400 MGL in size, did not submit in due time in the manner prescribed by legal acts to a state-authorised institution a declaration or a report or other document confirmed in the prescribed manner on a person's income, profit or property after this state institution reminded in writing of the obligation to submit them,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, where the amount of taxes or other contributions exceeds 900 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis. Fraudulent maintenance and (or) organisation of financial accounting

1.	A person who fraudulently maintained and (or) organised financial accounting required by legal acts or did not keep financial accounting documents and (or) financial accounting registers for the period prescribed by laws, or concealed, destroyed or damaged financial accounting documents and (or) financial accounting registers, or did not maintain and (or) did not organise financial accounting required by legal acts, if as a result major property damage was caused to the state or to a natural or legal person or it is impossible to fully or partially establish the size or structure of a person's activities, their property, equity or obligations,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article causing very major property damage to the state or to a natural or legal person,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

straipsnis. Negligent maintenance and (or) organisation of financial accounting

1.	A person who was obliged to maintain and (or) organise, but did not maintain and (or) did not organise financial accounting required by legal acts or negligently maintained and (or) organised financial accounting required by legal acts, or did not keep financial accounting documents and (or) financial accounting registers for the period prescribed by laws, if as a result major property damage was caused to the state or to a natural or legal person or it is impossible to fully or partially establish the size or structure of a person's activities, their property, equity or obligations,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
3.	A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through negligence.
Straipsnio pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-692, 2021-11-23, paskelbta TAR 2021-12-03,
i. k. 2021-25119
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

straipsnis

Production, possession or realisation of counterfeit or forged postage stamps, travel or other tickets, tax stamps or other official marking signs
1.	A person who produced, possessed or realised counterfeit or forged postage stamps, travel or other tickets, tax stamps or other official marking signs,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who produced, possessed or realised a large quantity or large value of counterfeit or forged postage stamps, travel or other tickets, tax stamps or other official marking signs,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Papildyta straipsnio dalimi:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

2241 straipsnis. Explanation of concepts
1.	The electronic means of payment referred to in Articles 214 and 215 of this Chapter is a protected tangible or intangible device, object or data or a combination thereof, allowing its holder or user to initiate or perform a financial transaction directly or together with one or more procedures, including transactions initiated or performed with electronic money or virtual currency.
2.	Property obtained by criminal means referred to in Article 216 of this Chapter is property of any form obtained directly or indirectly from a criminal act.
3.	Property damage, property benefit referred to in this Chapter is major when its size exceeds the amount of 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL, very major when its size exceeds the amount of 900 MGL.
Straipsnio dalies pakeitimai:
Nr. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, paskelbta TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

4.	Inside information referred to in Article 217 of this Chapter is understood as it is defined in Article 7(1)–(4) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 April 2014 on market abuse (Market Abuse Regulation) and repealing Directive 2003/6/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council and Commission Directives 2003/124/EC, 2003/125/EC and 2004/72/EC, and may be publicly disclosed only in the manner prescribed in Article 17 of this Regulation.
5.	Financial instruments referred to in Article 218 of this Chapter also include commodity derivatives relating to financial instruments, which are understood as they are defined in point 15 of Article 3(1) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014.
6.	A benchmark referred to in Article 218 of this Chapter is understood as it is defined in point 29 of Article 3(1) of Regulation (EU) No 596/2014.

XXXIII	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST PUBLIC SERVICE AND PUBLIC INTERESTS

This excerpt sits under: Suvestinė redakcija nuo 2024-01-01

Article. Bribery

1.	A public official or a person equated thereto who, for his or her own or others' benefit, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has promised or agreed to accept a bribe or has demanded or provoked the giving of a bribe, or has accepted a bribe for lawful action or inaction in the exercise of powers,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A public official or a person equated thereto who, for his or her own or others' benefit, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has promised or agreed to accept a bribe or has demanded or provoked the giving of a bribe, or has accepted a bribe for unlawful action or inaction in the exercise of powers,

shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
3.	A public official or a person equated thereto who, for his or her own or others' benefit, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has promised or agreed to accept or has demanded or provoked the giving of, or has accepted a bribe exceeding 250 MGL in value for lawful or unlawful action or inaction in the exercise of powers,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to eight years.
4.	A public official or a person equated thereto who, for his or her own or others' benefit, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has promised or agreed to accept or has demanded or provoked the giving of, or has accepted a bribe of less than 1 MGL in value for lawful or unlawful action or inaction in the exercise of powers, has committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or arrest.
5.	A public official or a person equated thereto shall be liable under this Code for a promise or agreement to accept a bribe or a demand or provocation to give a bribe, or the acceptance of a bribe both for a specific action or inaction in the exercise of powers and for an exceptional position or favour. Supplemented by a part of the article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

6.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendment to the numbering of the part of the article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

Amendments to the article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)
Amendments to the article:
No. XII-2780, 2016-11-10, published TAR 2016-11-16,
i. k. 2016-26876

Article. Trading in influence

1.	A person who, seeking that a person, taking advantage of his or her social position, service, powers, kinship, acquaintances or other credible influence on a State or municipal institution or establishment, an international public organisation, their public official or a person equated thereto, would influence the relevant institution, establishment or organisation, public official or person equated thereto, so that they would lawfully or unlawfully act or refrain from acting in the exercise of powers, has directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, offered, promised or agreed to give or has given a bribe to him or her or to a third person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four
years.
2.	A person who, taking advantage of his or her social position, service, powers, kinship,
acquaintances or other credible or alleged influence on a State or municipal institution or establishment, an international public organisation, their public official or a person equated thereto, for his or her own or others' benefit, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has promised or agreed to accept a bribe or has demanded or provoked the giving of a bribe, or has accepted a bribe, having promised to influence the relevant institution, establishment or organisation, public official or person equated thereto, so that they would lawfully or unlawfully act or refrain from acting in the exercise of powers,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having offered, promised or agreed to give or having given a bribe exceeding 250 MGL in value,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Amendments to the part of the article:
No. XIII-653, 2017-09-28, published TAR 2017-10-05,
i. k. 2017-15859

4.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 2 of this Article, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having promised or agreed to accept or having demanded or provoked the giving of, or having accepted a bribe exceeding 250 MGL in value,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to eight years.

5.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having offered, promised or agreed to give or having given, or having promised or agreed to accept, or having demanded or provoked the giving of, or having accepted a bribe of less than 1 MGL in value, has committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
6.	A person who has committed an act provided for in paragraph 1, 3 or 5 of this Article shall be released from criminal liability if a bribe was demanded from him or her or he or she was provoked to give a bribe and he or she, having directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, offered or promised to give or having given a bribe, reported it to a law enforcement institution within the shortest possible time, but not later than before his or her recognition as a suspect, and shall also be released from criminal liability if he or she promised to give or gave a bribe with the knowledge of a law enforcement institution.
7.	Paragraph 6 of this Article shall not apply to a person who directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, offered or promised to give or gave a bribe to a person referred to in paragraph 2 of Article 230 of this Code.
8.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2, 3, 4 and 5 of this Article.

No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Bribery

1.	A person who, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has offered, promised or agreed to give or has given a bribe to a public official or a person equivalent thereto or to a third person for the desired lawful action or inaction of a public official or a person equivalent thereto in the exercise of powers,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has offered, promised or agreed to give or has given a bribe to a public official or a person equivalent thereto or to a third person for the desired unlawful action or inaction of a public official or a person equivalent thereto in the exercise of powers,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having offered, promised or agreed to give or having given a bribe of a value exceeding 250 MGL,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-653, 2017-09-28, published TAR 2017-10-05,
i. k. 2017-15859

4.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having offered, promised or agreed to give or having given a bribe of a value less than 1 MGL, has committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.
5.	A person who has committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 of this Article shall be liable under this Code for seeking by means of a bribe both a specific action or inaction of a public official or a person equivalent thereto in the exercise of powers, and an exceptional position or favour of that person, regardless of how his actions were understood by the public official or a person equivalent thereto.
Supplemented by a paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

6.	A person shall be released from criminal liability for bribery if a bribe was demanded from him or he was provoked to give a bribe and he, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, having offered or promised to give or having given a bribe, within the shortest possible time, but not later than before his recognition as a suspect, voluntarily reported it to a law enforcement institution, and also if he promised to give or gave the bribe with the knowledge of a law enforcement institution.

Amendment to the numbering of the paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

7.	Paragraph 6 of this Article shall not apply to a person who, directly or indirectly, personally or through an intermediary, has offered or promised to give or has given a bribe to a person referred to in paragraph 2 of Article 230 of this Code.
Amendments to the paragraph of the Article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

8.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2, 3 and 4 of this Article.

Article. Abuse of Office

1.	A public official or a person equivalent thereto who has abused his official position or exceeded his powers, if as a result the state, the European Union, an international public organisation, a legal or natural person has suffered major damage,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A person who has committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article seeking pecuniary or other personal benefit, if there were no elements of bribery,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
3.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)
2281 Article. Unlawful Registration of Rights to a Thing
A public official or a person equivalent thereto who, while performing the functions of a registrar in a public register, has unlawfully registered rights to a thing,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
The Code supplemented by an Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Non-performance of Official Duties

A public official or a person equivalent thereto who, through negligence, has failed to perform his duties or has performed them improperly, if as a result the state, the European Union, an international public organisation, a legal or natural person has suffered major damage,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Explanation of Concepts

1.	The civil servants referred to in this Chapter are state politicians, state officials, judges, civil servants under the Civil Service Law and other persons who, while working or on other grounds provided by law holding positions in state or municipal institutions or establishments, perform the functions of a representative of the state or have administrative powers, as well as official candidates for these positions.

2.	A person equivalent to a civil servant is a person who, regardless of his or her status under the laws of a foreign state or international public organisation, performs the functions of a representative of the state, including judicial functions, has administrative powers or otherwise ensures the implementation of the public interest while working or on other grounds holding a position in a foreign state or European Union institution or establishment, an international public organisation or an international or European Union judicial institution, or in a legal person or other organisation controlled by a foreign state, as well as official candidates for these positions. A foreign state is considered to be any foreign territory, regardless of its legal status, and includes all levels and areas of governance.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XII-2048, 2015-11-19, published TAR 2015-11-25,
i. k. 2015-18711
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

3.	In addition, a person equivalent to a civil servant is a person who works or on other grounds provided by law holds a position in a public or private legal person or other organisation or engages in professional activity and has the relevant administrative powers, or has the right to act on behalf of that legal person or other organisation, or provides public services, as well as an arbitrator or a notary.
4.	The bribe referred to in this Chapter is an illegal or unjustified remuneration expressed in the form of any property or other personal benefit for oneself or another person (material or immaterial, having economic value in the market or not having such value) for the desired lawful or unlawful action or inaction of a civil servant or a person equivalent to him or her in the exercise of powers.
5.	The exercise of powers referred to in this Chapter includes any use of the position of a civil servant or a person equivalent to him or her, regardless of whether it falls within the scope of powers defined by legal acts for a civil servant or a person equivalent to him or her, or not.
Supplemented by a part of the Article:
No. XIII-391, 2017-06-01, published TAR 2017-06-02,
i. k. 2017-09472

6.	For the purposes of application of the provisions of Article 72 of this Code, the result of the prohibited acts in Article 226(1, 3, 5) and Article 227 of this Chapter is recognised as property of any form obtained directly or indirectly from these acts, including property benefit arising from the desired action or inaction of a civil servant or a person equivalent to him or her in the exercise of powers, regardless of whether it was obtained in the course of an activity that may be lawfully engaged in according to the procedure established by legal acts, or not. Supplemented by a part of the Article:
No. XII-2780, 2016-11-10, published TAR 2016-11-16,
i. k. 2016-26876
