XXXIV	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST JUSTICE

Article. Obstruction of the Activities of a Judge, Prosecutor, Pre-trial Investigation Officer,

Lawyer or Bailiff
1.	A person who in any way obstructed a judge, prosecutor, pre-trial investigation officer, lawyer or an official of the International Criminal Court or other international judicial institution in performing duties related to the investigation or examination of a criminal, civil, administrative or international judicial institution case, or obstructed a bailiff in executing a court decision,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using violence or other coercion,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.

3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Contempt of Court

A person who publicly by act, word or writing insultingly degraded a court or judge administering justice on account of their activities,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article

Influencing a Witness, Victim, Expert, Specialist or Interpreter
1.	A person who in any way sought to influence a witness, victim, expert, specialist or interpreter so that they would give false testimony, conclusions, explanations or translate incorrectly during pre-trial investigation, in court or in the International Criminal Court or other international judicial institution, or obstructed them from appearing before a pre-trial investigation officer, prosecutor, in court or in the International Criminal Court or other international judicial institution upon summons,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who in any way sought to influence a witness, expert, specialist or interpreter so that they would give false testimony, conclusions, explanations or translate incorrectly during impeachment proceedings to the Seimas special investigation commission or the Seimas, or obstructed them from appearing before the Seimas special investigation commission or the Seimas upon summons,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article by using violence or other coercion,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2093, 2004-03-30, Žin., 2004, No. 54-1835 (2004-04-15)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Influencing a Victim to Reconcile with the Offender

A person who sought to influence a victim, a representative of the state or a legal person so that they would reconcile with the offender, if this was done by using violence or other coercion,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.

straipsnis

False complaint, application, report, testimony, conclusion and translation
1. A person who submitted a false complaint, application, report about a criminal act or gave false testimony when questioned as a witness or victim, or being an expert or specialist submitted a false conclusion or explanation, or being an interpreter falsely or knowingly incorrectly translated during pre-trial investigation and (or) in court or in the International Criminal Court or other international judicial institution,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who during impeachment proceedings gave false testimony to the Seimas special investigation commission or the Seimas as a witness, being an expert or specialist submitted a false conclusion or explanation or being an interpreter falsely or knowingly incorrectly translated or submitted a false report, explanation, information or data to the Seimas temporary investigation commission or Seimas committee to which the Seimas granted the powers of a temporary investigation commission,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, accusing a person of allegedly committing a serious or very serious crime,

shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
4. A victim or witness shall not be held liable for giving false testimony if under the laws they had the right to refuse to give testimony, but before questioning they were not informed of this right.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2093, 2004-03-30, Žin., 2004, No. 54-1835 (2004-04-15)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
No. XI-521, 2009-12-03, Žin., 2009, No. 146-6484 (2009-12-10)
No. XI-976, 2010-06-30, Žin., 2010, No. 86-4528 (2010-07-20)

straipsnis. False accusation or report of a non-existent crime

1. A person who falsely accused to an institution or official having the right to initiate criminal prosecution an innocent person as having committed a criminal act, if as a result this person was subjected to prosecution, or reported a knowingly non-existent crime,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article and falsified evidence to initiate criminal prosecution of a person,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)

straipsnis. Concealment of a crime or a person who committed a crime

1. A person who without prior promise concealed, destroyed or damaged traces, instruments or means of a serious or very serious crime committed by another person, items acquired by criminal means, other things related to the concealed crime having evidentiary value, or concealed a person who committed a crime,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. Close relatives and family members of a person who committed a crime shall not be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis. Failure to report a crime

1. A person who without good reason failed to report to a law enforcement institution or court a very serious crime being committed or committed known to them,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.
2. Close relatives and family members of a person who committed that crime shall not be held liable for failure to report a crime.

straipsnis. Disorganisation of work of a place of deprivation of liberty

1. A person who being detained, remanded in custody or serving a sentence of arrest, imprisonment or life imprisonment terrorised another person or persons held in a place of deprivation of liberty,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2. A person who being detained, remanded in custody or serving a sentence of arrest, imprisonment or life imprisonment organised riots of other persons held in a place of deprivation of liberty, if during them a person was seriously injured or died or large property damage was caused, or other serious consequences occurred, as well as a person who actively participated in such riots,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to fifteen years.

straipsnis. Release of a prisoner

A person who by using violence against guards or by abuse of trust, or by fraud released a person who is detained, remanded in custody or serving a sentence of arrest or imprisonment, or life imprisonment,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.

straipsnis. Escape of a prisoner

1. A person who being detained, remanded in custody or serving a sentence of arrest, imprisonment or life imprisonment escaped from their place of detention,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using violence against guards or other persons or by causing large property damage to their detention institution,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

straipsnis

Evasion of serving a sentence of arrest, imprisonment or returning to a place of remand in custody
A person who being sentenced to a sentence of arrest or imprisonment, but not yet imprisoned evaded serving the imposed sentence or being temporarily released from an arrest house or place of imprisonment or remand in custody without good reason did not return at the appointed time,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

straipsnis. Evasion of serving penal impact measures

A person who evaded serving a penal impact measure imposed by a court (except confiscation of property), committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or arrest.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-2263, 2019-06-27, published TAR 2019-07-05,
i. k. 2019-11177

straipsnis. Non-execution of a penalty imposed on a legal person

An employee of a legal person responsible for the execution of a penalty imposed on this legal person, who failed to execute it, committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest.

straipsnis. Non-execution of a court decision not related to a penalty

A person who failed to execute a court decision not related to penalties, committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.

straipsnis

Transfer, concealment, destruction or damage of described or arrested property or property subject to a temporary restriction of ownership rights
1. A person who concealed, destroyed or damaged described, arrested property entrusted to them or property subject to a temporary restriction of ownership rights, or unlawfully transferred this property to another person,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who concealed, destroyed or damaged described, arrested property of large value entrusted to them or property of large value subject to a temporary restriction of ownership rights, or unlawfully transferred this property to another person,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
3. A person who concealed, destroyed or damaged described, arrested property of very large
value entrusted to them or property of very large value subject to a temporary restriction of ownership rights, or unlawfully transferred this property to another person,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

straipsnis. Disclosure of pre-trial investigation data without permission

A person who before the examination of a case in a trial hearing without the permission of the judge, prosecutor, pre-trial investigation officer investigating this case disclosed pre-trial investigation data, committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.

This excerpt sits under: Suvestinė redakcija nuo 2024-01-01

Article. Interpretation of Concepts

1.	Close relatives are parents (adoptive parents), children (adopted children), brothers, sisters, grandparents and grandchildren.
2.	Law enforcement institutions are the police, other pre-trial investigation and prosecution institutions, as well as criminal intelligence entities.
3.	Property provided for in this chapter is of large value when its value exceeds the amount of 400 MGL but does not exceed the amount of 900 MGL, of very large value when its value exceeds the amount of 900 MGL.
4.	Property damage referred to in this chapter is large when its amount exceeds the amount of 400 MGL.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

XXXV	CHAPTER
CRIMES AGAINST PUBLIC SECURITY

Article. Criminal Association

1.	A person who participated in the activities of a criminal association,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years.
2.	A person who participated in the activities of a criminal association armed with firearms, explosives or explosive substances,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of six to twenty years or by deprivation of liberty for life.
3.	A person who organised the criminal associations provided for in paragraphs 1 or 2 of this Article
or led them,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years or by deprivation of liberty for life.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. IX-1706, 2003-07-04, Žin., 2003, No. 74-3423 (2003-07-25)
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)

2491 Article. Creation and Activities of Groups Whose Purpose is to Commit Terrorist Crimes
1.	A person who created an organised group whose purpose is to commit terrorist crimes, or who
led it or participated in the activities of this group,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.
2.	A person who participated in the activities of a terrorist group whose purpose is to commit terrorist crimes,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to fifteen years.
3.	A person who participated in the activities of a terrorist group armed with firearms, explosives, explosive, radioactive, biological or chemical harmful substances, preparations or microorganisms,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years or by deprivation of liberty for life.
4.	A person who created a terrorist group provided for in paragraphs 2 or 3 of this Article or led it,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of twelve to twenty years or by deprivation of liberty for life.
5.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:

No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Terrorist Act

1.	A person who, for terrorist purposes, manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, transferred or otherwise disposed of a firearm, ammunition, explosives, explosive, nuclear or radioactive substances, other sources of ionising radiation, as well as a person who, for terrorist purposes, created, manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, transferred or otherwise disposed of a chemical or biological weapon or chemical substances or their precursors used for the manufacture of chemical or biological weapons, microorganisms, other biological substances or toxins,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
2.	A person who, for terrorist purposes, caused a flood or disrupted the supply of water, energy or other natural resources, or exploded, set fire to or otherwise destroyed or damaged property on a large scale, or affected an information system or electronic data, or disseminated radioactive, biological or chemical harmful substances, preparations or microorganisms, if this resulted in or could have resulted in serious consequences, as well as a person who, for terrorist purposes, impaired the health or caused physical pain to one or more persons or caused danger to the life or health of many persons, or affected an information system or its electronic data of major importance to state governance, the economy or the financial system,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-1682, 2018-12-04, published TAR 2018-12-11,
i. k. 2018-20234
No. XIV-1451, 2022-10-11, published TAR 2022-10-19,
i. k. 2022-21166

3.	A person who, for terrorist purposes, seriously impaired the health of one or more persons, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to fifteen years.
4.	A person who, for terrorist purposes, killed one or more persons,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of eight to twenty years or by imprisonment for life.
5.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraphs 2, 3 or 4 of this Article, if it was directed against an object of strategic importance to national security or if it resulted in very serious consequences,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by imprisonment for life.
6.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)
Article 2501. Incitement to Terrorist Offences
1.	A person who publicly encouraged or incited the commission of terrorist offences or vilified the victims of such offences,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:
No. IX-2570, 2004-11-11, Žin., 2004, No. 171-6318 (2004-11-26)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)

Article 2502. Recruitment for Terrorist Activity
1.	A person who recruited another person to commit a terrorist offence or to participate in the commission of a terrorist offence or to participate in the activities of a group whose purpose is to commit terrorist offences,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)

Article 2503. Threat to Commit a Terrorist Offence

1.	A person who threatened to commit a terrorist offence referred to in Articles 250, 251 or 252 of this Code, if there was sufficient ground to believe that the threat could be carried out,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-1682, 2018-12-04, published TAR 2018-12-11,
i. k. 2018-20234

2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)
Article 2504. Financing and Support of Terrorist Activity
1.	A person who directly or indirectly collected, accumulated or provided funds or other property or provided other material support to another person, knowing or intending that such property, support or part thereof would be used to prepare or commit a terrorist or terrorist-related offence or to support one or more terrorists or a group whose purpose is to commit terrorist offences, or persons or groups who recruit, train terrorists or otherwise participate in terrorist activity,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-342, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08023

2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)

Article 2505. Training of Terrorists and Learning for Terrorist Purposes
1.	A person who provided another person with special knowledge or skills necessary to prepare, commit a terrorist offence or to participate in the commission of a terrorist offence, knowing that the person intends to use the knowledge or skills provided for terrorist purposes, as well as a person who, in order to use the knowledge or skills acquired for terrorist purposes, systematically collected special knowledge or acquired special skills necessary to prepare, commit a terrorist offence or to participate in the commission of a terrorist offence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code has been supplemented with an Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-342, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08023

Article 2506. Travel for Terrorist Purposes
1.	A person who arrived in the Republic of Lithuania or travelled to another state, in order to prepare or commit a terrorist offence or to participate in the commission of a terrorist offence, or to participate in the activities of a group whose purpose is to commit terrorist offences, or to train terrorists, or to learn for terrorist purposes,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented with an Article:
No. XIII-342, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08023

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article

Hijacking of an aircraft, ship or other public or cargo transport vehicle or stationary platform on the continental shelf
1.	A person who hijacked an aircraft, ship or other public or cargo transport vehicle or a
stationary platform on the continental shelf,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1451, 2022-10-11, published TAR 2022-10-19,
i. k. 2022-21166

2.	A person who, by using physical violence or threatening with violence, hijacked an aircraft, ship or other public or cargo transport vehicle or a stationary platform on the continental shelf,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1451, 2022-10-11, published TAR 2022-10-19,
i. k. 2022-21166

3.	A person who, by using a firearm, explosive or other means dangerous to the life or health of the crew or passengers of an aircraft, ship or other public or cargo transport vehicle or persons on a stationary platform on the continental shelf, hijacked an aircraft, ship or other public or cargo transport vehicle or a stationary platform on the continental shelf,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to ten years.
4.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 of this Article for terrorist purposes shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.
5.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1, 2, 3 or 4 of this Article, if as a result thereof an incident, accident or other very serious consequences occurred,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by imprisonment for life.
6.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)
Article 2511. Piracy
1.	A crew member or passenger of a civil ship or aircraft who, seeking pecuniary or other personal gain, on the high seas or in another territory where no state jurisdiction applies, unlawfully detained another ship or aircraft, a person, a group of persons or property of others located on that ship or aircraft, or used other physical or mental coercion against that person or group of persons,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
2.	A crew member of a warship, state ship or aircraft who instigated a mutiny during which control of the ship or aircraft was seized, or participated in such mutiny and committed an act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, or a person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using a firearm, explosive or other tool or means dangerous to human life or health,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to ten years.
3.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 or 2 of this Article, if as a result thereof very serious consequences occurred,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of ten to twenty years or by imprisonment for life.
4.	A person who used a ship or aircraft, knowing that the acts provided for in paragraph 1, 2 or 3 of this Article were committed with it and that control of the ship or aircraft belongs to the persons who committed these acts,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
5.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented by Article:
No. XII-776, 2014-03-13, published TAR 2014-03-24,
i. k. 2014-03404

Article. Hostage taking

1.	A person who, for terrorist purposes, abducted or held an abducted person, as well as a person who threatened to immediately kill or injure an abducted person demanding that conditions be created to avoid detention,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1451, 2022-10-11, published TAR 2022-10-19,
i. k. 2022-21166

2.	A person who committed an act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, if he abducted or held abducted two or more persons,

shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article 2521. Explanation of concepts
1.	Terrorist crimes are the crime specified in Article 250 of this Code and the crimes specified in Articles 2503, 251 and 252 of this Code committed for terrorist purposes. Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-1682, 2018-12-04, published TAR 2018-12-11,
i. k. 2018-20234

2.	Crimes related to terrorist activity are the crimes specified in Articles 2491, 2501, 2502, 2504, 2505 and
2506 of this Code, as well as the crimes specified in Articles 178, 180, 181 and 300 of this Code, if they are aimed at obtaining funds, tools or means for committing terrorist crimes or supporting the activities of a group whose purpose is to commit terrorist crimes.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-342, 2017-05-04, published TAR 2017-05-11,
i. k. 2017-08023
No. XIII-1682, 2018-12-04, published TAR 2018-12-11,
i. k. 2018-20234

3.	Terrorist purposes – the aim to seriously intimidate society or a part thereof or to unlawfully compel an international public organisation, a state or its institution to perform any action or to refrain from performing it, or to destabilise or destroy the most important constitutional, political, economic or social structures of the state or an international public organisation.
The Code is supplemented by Article:
No. XII-497, 2013-07-02, Žin., 2013, No. 75-3768 (2013-07-13)
XXXVI	CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS RELATED TO DISPOSAL OF WEAPONS, AMMUNITION, EXPLOSIVES, EXPLOSIVE OR
RADIOACTIVE SUBSTANCES OR MILITARY EQUIPMENT
Chapter title amended:
No. IX-2512, 2004-10-28, Žin., 2004, No. 166-6061 (2004-11-16)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article

Illegal Handling of Firearms, Ammunition, Explosives or Explosive Substances
1.	A person who, without authorisation, manufactured, acquired, possessed, carried, transported, sent, used or disposed of a firearm, ammunition, explosives or explosive substances,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A person who, without authorisation, manufactured, acquired, possessed, carried, transported, sent or disposed of not less than three firearms, high explosive power or a large quantity of ammunition, explosives or explosive substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article 253(1). Illegal Intermediation in the Transfer of Military Equipment
1.	A person who, without authorisation, acted as an intermediary in the transfer of military equipment to another, non-European Union, state,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an article:
No. IX-2512, 2004-10-28, Žin., 2004, No. 166-6061 (2004-11-16)
Amendments to the Article:

No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article 2532. Smuggling of Firearms, Ammunition, Explosives, Explosive Substances or Strategic Goods
1.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without authorisation, transported or sent across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania a firearm, ammunition, explosives, explosive substances or strategic goods,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without authorisation, transported or sent across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania not less than three firearms, high explosive power or a large quantity of ammunition, explosives or explosive substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to ten years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented with an article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article

Seizure of a Firearm, Ammunition, Explosives or Explosive Substances
1.	A person who seized a firearm, ammunition, explosives or explosive substances shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2.	A person who, using physical or mental violence, seized a firearm, ammunition, explosives or explosive substances or who, by any means, seized more than two firearms, high explosive power or a large quantity of ammunition, explosives or explosive substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to ten years.

Article

Violation of the Rules for Possession, Carrying or Transportation of a Firearm, Ammunition, Explosives or Explosive Substances
Amended title of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

1.	A person who violated the rules for possession, carrying or transportation of a lawfully held firearm, ammunition, explosives or explosive substances and as a result another person unlawfully used them or as a result the firearm, ammunition, explosives or explosive substances were lost, shall commit a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

2.	A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for were committed through negligence.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article

Illegal Handling of Nuclear or Radioactive Substances or Other Sources of Ionising Radiation
1.	A person who seized or otherwise unlawfully acquired, manufactured, transported, sent, possessed, stored, processed, used, destroyed, disposed of or otherwise unlawfully handled nuclear or radioactive substances or other sources of ionising radiation of any form and any physical state,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, transported, sent, possessed, stored, processed, used, destroyed, disposed of or otherwise unlawfully handled nuclear or radioactive substances or other sources of ionising radiation of any form and any physical state, if this resulted in serious consequences,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years.
3.	The act provided for in paragraph 2 of this Article shall be criminal also in cases where it was committed through negligence.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article 256(1). Threat to Use or Otherwise Affect or Unlawfully Acquire Nuclear or Radioactive Substances or Other Sources of Ionising Radiation
1.	A person who threatened to use or otherwise affect nuclear or radioactive substances or other sources of ionising radiation of any form and any physical state so that they would pose a danger to human life, health or the environment, or threatened to seize or otherwise unlawfully acquire these sources,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article 2562. Smuggling of Nuclear or Radioactive Substances or Other Sources of Ionising Radiation
1.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without authorisation, transported or sent across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania nuclear or radioactive substances or other sources of ionising radiation,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Supplemented with an article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article

Violation of the rules for lawful handling of nuclear or radioactive materials or other sources of ionising radiation
1. A person who violated the rules for storage, use or transportation of nuclear or radioactive materials of any form and any physical state or other sources of ionising radiation, if serious consequences could have resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for an act provided for in this Article.
3. An act provided for in this Article shall be recognised as criminal also in cases where it
was committed through negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

257(1) Article. Manufacture of devices for the production of explosive substances, explosives or radioactive materials or preparation or distribution of their production technologies or instructions
1. A person who unlawfully manufactured, stored, transported, transferred or sold apparatus or other devices directly intended or adapted for the production of explosive substances, explosives or radioactive materials, or unlawfully prepared or distributed technologies or instructions for the production of explosive substances, explosives or radioactive materials,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Unlawful handling of non-firearm weapons

1. A person who unlawfully manufactured with the purpose of selling or sold a non-firearm weapon or other device adapted to harm people,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A person who unlawfully acquired or carried a non-firearm weapon or other object adapted to harm people, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest.

CHAPTER XXXVII
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS RELATED TO HANDLING OF NARCOTIC OR PSYCHOTROPIC, POISONOUS OR POTENT SUBSTANCES

Article

Unlawful handling of narcotic or psychotropic substances without the purpose of distributing them
1. A person who unlawfully manufactured, processed, acquired, stored, transported or sent narcotic or psychotropic substances without having the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who unlawfully manufactured, processed, acquired, stored, transported or sent a small quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances without having the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or by restriction of liberty, or by a fine, or by arrest.
3. A person who voluntarily applied to a health care institution for medical assistance or applied to a state institution wishing to surrender narcotic or psychotropic substances unlawfully manufactured, acquired or stored without the purpose of distributing them, shall be released from criminal liability for the manufacture, acquisition and storage of the narcotic or psychotropic substances used or surrendered.

Article

Unlawful handling of narcotic or psychotropic substances with the purpose of distributing them or unlawful handling of a very large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances
1. A person who unlawfully manufactured, processed, acquired, stored, transported or sent narcotic or psychotropic substances with the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them or sold or otherwise distributed narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between two and eight years.
2. A person who unlawfully manufactured, processed, acquired, stored, transported or sent a large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances with the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them or sold or otherwise distributed a large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between eight and ten years.
3. A person who unlawfully manufactured, processed, acquired, stored, transported, sent, sold or otherwise distributed a very large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between ten and fifteen years.
4. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)
2601 Article. Smuggling of narcotic or psychotropic substances
1. A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or sent a small quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania without having the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or sent narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania without having the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them,

shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3. A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or sent narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania with the purpose of selling or otherwise distributing them,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between two and ten years.
4. A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or sent a large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between four and twelve years.
5. A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or sent a very large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between ten and eighteen years.
6. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in parts 3, 4 and 5 of this Article.
7. A person who voluntarily applied to a health care institution for medical assistance or to a state institution wishing to surrender a small quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances unlawfully transported or sent without the purpose of distributing them, shall be released from criminal liability for the smuggling of the narcotic or psychotropic substances surrendered.
Supplemented with an article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances to minors

A person who distributed narcotic or psychotropic substances to minors, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of between three and twelve years.

Article

Manufacture of devices for the production of narcotic or psychotropic substances or preparation of technologies or instructions for the production of narcotic or psychotropic substances
A person who unlawfully manufactured, stored, transported or sold apparatus or other devices for the production of narcotic or psychotropic substances or unlawfully prepared or distributed technologies or instructions for the production of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article

Theft, extortion or other unlawful seizure of narcotic or psychotropic substances
1.	A person who stole, seized by fraud (swindling) or misappropriated narcotic or psychotropic substances entrusted to him or in his possession,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A person who extorted or seized narcotic or psychotropic substances by using physical or mental violence,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
3.	A person who, by the acts specified in paragraphs 1 or 2 of this Article, seized a large quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances or seized narcotic or psychotropic substances while participating in an organised group,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of five to fifteen years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Inducement to use narcotic or psychotropic substances

1.	A person who helped a person to acquire, forced, induced or otherwise habituated him to use narcotic or psychotropic substances for non-medical purposes,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

2.	A person who helped a minor to acquire, forced, induced or otherwise habituated him to use narcotic or psychotropic substances for non-medical purposes,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.

Article. Unlawful cultivation of poppies or cannabis

1.	A person who, in violation of the established procedure, cultivated a large quantity of poppies, cannabis or other plants included in the list of narcotic and psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)

Article

Unlawful handling of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances
1.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched or sold or otherwise disposed of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched or sold or otherwise disposed of a large quantity of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to six years.
3.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched or sold or otherwise disposed of a very large quantity of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of six to ten years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-711, 2006-06-22, Žin., 2006, No. 77-2961 (2006-07-14)
No. XI-974, 2010-06-30, Žin., 2010, No. 86-4527 (2010-07-20)
2661 Article. Smuggling of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances
1.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or dispatched category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or dispatched a large quantity of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to seven years.
3.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or dispatched a very large quantity of category one precursors of narcotic or psychotropic substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of seven to twelve years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented by Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article

Unlawful handling of potent or poisonous substances
1.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched or disposed of potent or poisonous substances,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who unlawfully manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched or disposed of poisonous chemical substances used as chemical weapons, chemical substances or their precursors for the manufacture of chemical weapons or for other purposes prohibited by the Chemical Weapons Prohibition Act,

shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

267(1) Article. Development or unlawful handling of biological weapons
1.	A person who unlawfully developed, manufactured, acquired, possessed, transported, dispatched, sold or otherwise transferred biological weapons or microorganisms or other biological substances or toxins used for their manufacture,
shall be punished by a fine or by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.

1.	A person who, without submitting to customs control or otherwise evading it or without having a permit, transported or dispatched potent or poisonous substances across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or by restriction of liberty, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to four

permit, transported or dispatched poisonous chemical substances used as chemical weapons, or chemical substances or their precursors used for the manufacture of chemical weapons, across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.

Article

Violation of the rules for lawful handling of narcotic, psychotropic, potent or poisonous substances
1.	A person who violated the rules for the manufacture, possession, accounting, dispensing, transportation or dispatching of narcotic, psychotropic, potent or poisonous substances, if as a result those substances were seized or otherwise became part of illegal circulation,
shall be punished by community service or by a fine, or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
3.	A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed
through negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Interpretation of Concepts

1. The narcotic and psychotropic substances specified in this chapter are substances included in the lists of narcotic and psychotropic substances approved by the Ministry of Health of the Republic of Lithuania.
2. What quantity of narcotic or psychotropic substances shall be considered small, what quantity large and very large, shall be determined on the basis of recommendations approved by the Ministry of Health of the Republic of Lithuania.
3. What quantity of category I precursors of narcotic and psychotropic substances shall be considered large and very large, shall be determined on the basis of recommendations approved by an institution authorised by the Government of the Republic of Lithuania, responsible for the licensing of activities related to category I precursors of narcotic and psychotropic substances.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-974, 2010-06-30, Žin., 2010, No. 86-4527 (2010-07-20)
XXXVIII CHAPTER
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE ENVIRONMENT AND HUMAN HEALTH

Article

Violation of the Rules for Environmental Protection or Use of Natural Resources or Supervision or Use of Structures in which Hazardous Substances are Used or Stored or which Contain Potentially Dangerous Equipment or in which Dangerous Activities are Carried Out
1. A person who violated the rules for environmental protection or use of natural resources, or supervision or use of structures in which hazardous substances are used or stored or which contain potentially dangerous equipment or in which dangerous activities are carried out, established by legal acts, if this created a danger to human life or health or could have caused significant damage to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components or other serious consequences to any of these environmental elements,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-2152, 2023-07-04, published in TAR 2023-07-11,
i. k. 2023-14338

2. A person who committed the act specified in paragraph 1 of this Article or systematically violated the rules for environmental protection or use of natural resources, or supervision or use of structures in which hazardous substances are used or stored or which contain potentially dangerous equipment or in which dangerous activities are carried out, established by legal acts, if this caused significant damage to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components or other serious consequences to any of these environmental elements,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIV-2152, 2023-07-04, published in TAR 2023-07-11,
i. k. 2023-14338

3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
4. The acts provided for in this Article shall be criminal also in cases where they were committed through negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XI-1901, 2011-12-22, Žin., 2011, No. 163-7777 (2011-12-31)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-2558, 2019-11-28, published in TAR 2019-12-10,
i. k. 2019-19866

2701 Article. Illegal Handling of Ozone-Depleting Substances or their Mixtures
1. A person who illegally produced, imported, exported, used or placed on the market ozone-depleting substances or their mixtures,

shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1901, 2011-12-22, Žin., 2011, No. 163-7777 (2011-12-31)
2702 Article. Illegal Transportation of Waste across the State Border of the Republic of Lithuania
1. A person who, without a permit or otherwise violating the requirements of legal acts, transported across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania fifty tonnes or a larger quantity of non-hazardous waste intended for use or disposal,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A person who, without a permit or otherwise violating the requirements of legal acts, transported across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania fifty tonnes or a larger quantity of hazardous waste intended for use or disposal,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an Article:
No. XI-1901, 2011-12-22, Žin., 2011, No. 163-7777 (2011-12-31)
2703 Article. Marine Pollution from Ships
1. A person who, in violation of the requirements established in Annexes I and II of the International Convention for the Prevention of Pollution from Ships (MARPOL 73/78), polluted from ships the high seas or another territory not subject to the jurisdiction of any state, the exclusive economic zone, territorial sea, straits used for international navigation subject to the jurisdiction of these states, internal waters, including ports, of the Republic of Lithuania or other Member States of the European Union, if this resulted in deterioration of water quality,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. The act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be criminal also in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.
Supplemented with an Article:
No. XII-1777, 2015-06-11, published in TAR 2015-06-18,
i. k. 2015-09697

2704 Article. Discharge of Waste into the Environment
1. A person who illegally discharged into the environment 15 cubic metres or more of non-hazardous waste (except for illegal use of inert waste), if this did not cause significant damage to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components and did not result in other serious consequences to any of these environmental elements,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who illegally discharged into the environment 7 cubic metres or more of hazardous waste, if this did not cause significant damage to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components and did not result in other serious consequences to any of these environmental elements,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four
years.
3. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Supplemented with an Article:
No. XIV-2152, 2023-07-04, published in TAR 2023-07-11,
i. k. 2023-14338

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Destruction or Damage of Protected Territories or Natural Heritage Objects

1.	A person who destroyed or damaged a state park, reserve, sanctuary, or other state-protected natural territory, a protected natural or species habitat located therein, or a natural heritage territorial complex for the protection purposes of which this territory was established, or a natural heritage object,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five
years.
2.	The acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article are criminal also in cases where they
were committed through negligence.
3.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XI-1901, 2011-12-22, Žin., 2011, No. 163-7777 (2011-12-31)
2711 Article. Violation of Legal Requirements Regulating Construction
1.	A person who carried out unauthorised construction or otherwise violated the requirements of legal acts regulating construction, if as a result significant damage was caused to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components or other serious consequences occurred to any of these environmental elements,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	The act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article is criminal also in cases where it was committed through negligence.
3.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XIV-2152, 2023-07-04, published in TAR 2023-07-11,
i. k. 2023-14338

Article

Illegal Hunting or Fishing or Other Use of Wildlife Resources
1.	A person who hunted or fished at a prohibited time, in prohibited places or by prohibited methods, tools, means or otherwise illegally used or destroyed wildlife resources and caused significant damage to fauna, as well as a person who fished using electric or ultrasound fishing devices,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Article paragraph amendments:
No. XII-1871, 2015-06-25, published in TAR 2015-07-10,
i. k. 2015-11240

2.	A person who illegally destroyed, damaged or took from the natural environment wild animals of specially protected species, acquired, kept, transported, sold or used for commercial purposes wild animals of specially protected species, their parts or products made from them, if such an act did not cause significant damage to the conservation status of the species of wild animals of specially protected species and such an act was related to a small quantity of wild animals of specially protected species,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A person who illegally destroyed, damaged, took from the natural environment, caught or otherwise acquired, kept, processed, transported, sold or otherwise used a large quantity of wild animals of protected species, their parts or products made from them or such an act caused significant damage to the conservation status of the species of wild animals of protected species, or committed such an act for commercial purposes,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
4.	A person who illegally manufactured, acquired, kept, transported or sold electric or ultrasound fishing devices, commits a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
5.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
6.	The acts provided for in paragraphs 1, 2 and 3 of this Article are criminal also in cases where they
were committed through negligence.
Article amendments:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

Article. Illegal Felling of Forest or Destruction of Wetlands

1.	A person who, without having a permit, felled, destroyed or damaged more than 500 cubic metres of trees on forest land or drained a wetland,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Article amendments:
No. XII-1871, 2015-06-25, published in TAR 2015-07-10,
i. k. 2015-11240

Article. Illegal Picking, Destruction, Sale or Other Disposal of Protected Wild Plants, Fungi or Their Parts

1.	A person who illegally destroyed, damaged, took from the natural environment or otherwise picked or acquired, kept, processed, transported, sold or otherwise used a large quantity of wild plants, fungi of protected species, their parts or products made from them or such an act caused significant damage to the conservation status of the species of wild plants or fungi of protected species, or committed such an act for commercial purposes,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who illegally destroyed, damaged, took from the natural environment or otherwise picked wild plants, fungi of specially protected species, acquired, kept, transported, sold or used for commercial purposes wild plants, fungi of specially protected species, their parts or products made from them, if such an act did not cause significant damage to the conservation status of the species of wild plants or fungi of specially protected species and such an act was related to a small quantity of wild plants or fungi of specially protected species, commits a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine.
3.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
4.	The acts provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article are criminal also in cases where they
were committed through negligence.
Article amendments:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)
No. XI-579, 2009-12-17, Žin., 2010, No. 1-1 (2010-01-05)

Article. Illegal Pharmaceutical Activity

1.	A person who, without having a permit, manufactured medicines or medicinal substances intending to sell them, if their use could pose a danger to human health or life, as well as a person who sold these pharmaceutical products,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who, without having a permit, manufactured medicines or medicinal substances intending to sell them, as well as a person who sold these pharmaceutical products, if their use resulted in the death of a person or serious impairment of a person's health,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3.	The acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article are criminal also in cases where they were committed through negligence.
4.	A person shall be held liable under paragraph 2 of this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through negligence.
5.	A legal entity shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

straipsnis

Production or trade of products harmful to human health or life
1. A person who, with the intent to distribute, produced food products from obviously unsuitable substances harmful to human health or life or with harmful additives, as well as a person who sold or otherwise distributed such products,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who, with the intent to distribute, produced food products from obviously unsuitable substances harmful to human health or life or with harmful additives, as well as a person who sold or otherwise distributed such products, where their consumption resulted in the death of a person or serious impairment of a person's health,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3. The act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be criminal also in cases where it was committed through negligence.

4. A person shall be held liable under paragraph 2 of this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through negligence.
5. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Article 2761. Illegal handling of substances specified in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Control of Certain Doping Substances with the intent to distribute them
1. A person who, with the intent to sell or otherwise distribute, illegally produced, processed, acquired, possessed, transported or dispatched substances specified in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Control of Certain Doping Substances, or sold or otherwise distributed them,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

A person who distributed substances specified in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Control of Certain Doping Substances to minors for non-therapeutic purposes,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.

straipsnis. Violation of rules for combating epidemics or infectious diseases

1. A person who, having been informed by a medical institution about his illness and warned about the protective measures he must observe when communicating with people, created a danger for another person to contract a dangerous or particularly dangerous infectious disease,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.

shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
