Article 2771. Explanation of concepts
1. Specially protected wild animals, plants and fungi are:
1) animals, plants and fungi of species included in the List of Protected Animal, Plant and Fungal Species of the Republic of Lithuania pursuant to the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Protected Animal, Plant and Fungal Species;
2) animals, plants and fungi of species specified in Annexes A and B of Regulation (EC) No 338/97;
3) animals and plants of species included in the list of animal and plant species of Community importance requiring strict protection pursuant to the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Protected Animal, Plant and Fungal Species;
4) birds of species included in the list of Community bird species for whose habitats special protection measures are applied so that they are preserved, survive and breed in their natural range pursuant to the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Protected Animal, Plant and Fungal Species.
2. The dangerous substances referred to in Article 270 of this Code shall be understood as they are
defined in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Environmental Protection.
3. The potentially dangerous installations referred to in Article 270 of this Code shall be understood as they are defined in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on the Supervision of Potentially Dangerous Installations.
4. The non-hazardous waste and hazardous waste referred to in Article 2704 of this Code shall be
understood as they are defined in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Waste Management.
5. The inert waste referred to in Article 2704 of this Code is waste in which no noticeable chemical, physical or biological changes occur, which does not dissolve, does not burn or otherwise react physically or chemically, does not biodegrade and does not cause adverse effects on other substances with which it comes into contact that could lead to environmental pollution or harm to human health.
6. Damage to air, land, soil, water, animals, plants, other living organisms, organic and inorganic substances or anthropogenic components referred to in Articles 270, 2704 and 2711 of this Code shall be considered significant where it is established that significant adverse impact has been caused to a particular environmental element, as it is defined in the Law of the Republic of Lithuania on Environmental Protection, or the total amount of damage caused to one or more environmental elements, assessed in monetary terms, exceeds 400 MGL.

Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-2152, 2023-07-04, published TAR 2023-07-11,
i. k. 2023-14338

CHAPTER XXXIX
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST TRAFFIC SAFETY

This excerpt sits under: Suvestinė redakcija nuo 2024-01-01

Article

Improper maintenance or repair of vehicles or roads and equipment thereon
1. A person who improperly performed maintenance or repair of railway, water or air transport vehicles or roads, signalling or communication equipment on roads, gas pipelines, oil pipelines, electric or communication lines, if as a result a person was seriously injured or major property damage was caused,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, if as a result a person died or persons were seriously injured, or very major property damage was caused,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.
3. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, if as a result serious consequences did not occur but they were a real threat, committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest.

4. A person shall be held liable under paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through negligence.
5. The act provided for in paragraph 3 of this Article is criminal also in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Violation of international flight rules

A crew member of an aircraft or other flying device who violated international flight rules when flying into the Republic of Lithuania or flying out of the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article. Damage to vehicles or roads and equipment thereon

1. A person who dismantled or damaged a vehicle or road, gas pipeline, oil pipeline, electric or communication line, equipment thereon, communication or signalling device, if this threatened serious consequences,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who committed the actions specified in paragraph 1 of this Article, if as a result a person died or was seriously injured or major property damage was caused,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.
3. The acts provided for in this Article are criminal also in cases where they were committed through negligence.
4. A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-272, 2005-06-23, Žin., 2005, No. 81-2945 (2005-06-30)

Article

Violation of road traffic safety or vehicle operation rules
1. A person who while driving a road vehicle violated road traffic safety or vehicle operation rules, if as a result a traffic accident occurred due to which another person's health was slightly impaired,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who drove a road vehicle while intoxicated or under the influence of narcotic, psychotropic or other psychoactive substances and violated road traffic safety or vehicle operation rules, if as a result a traffic accident occurred due to which another person's health was slightly impaired or major property damage was caused to the injured person,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3. A person who while driving a road vehicle violated road traffic safety or vehicle operation rules, if as a result a traffic accident occurred due to which another person's health was seriously impaired,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
4. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 3 of this Article while intoxicated or under the influence of narcotic, psychotropic or other psychoactive substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
5. A person who while driving a road vehicle violated road traffic safety or vehicle operation rules, if as a result a traffic accident occurred due to which a person died,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
6. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 5 of this Article while intoxicated or under the influence of narcotic, psychotropic or other psychoactive substances,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
7. A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for in this Article were committed through negligence.
8. Expired as of 2023-06-01 Repeal of paragraph of the Article:

No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

9. Expired as of 2023-06-01 Repeal of paragraph of the Article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Amendments to the Article:
No. XIII-1929, 2019-01-11, published in TAR 2019-01-21,
i. k. 2019-00859

Article 2811. Driving of vehicles when driven by an intoxicated person
1. A person who drove a motor vehicle, tractor or self-propelled machine or taught practical driving while intoxicated, when intoxication of 1.51 or more per mille was established, or avoided an intoxication check when signs of intoxication were established, or consumed alcohol after a traffic accident until the establishment of its circumstances and intoxication of 1.51 or more per mille was established,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A person shall be held liable also in cases where the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article were committed through negligence.
Supplemented with an article:
No. XIII-1929, 2019-01-11, published in TAR 2019-01-21,
i. k. 2019-00859

Article. Violation of traffic order or safety rules

1. A person who without driving a vehicle violated traffic order or safety rules, if as a result a person died or a person's health was seriously impaired,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through
negligence.
Article 2821. Explanation of concepts
1. Property damage specified in this Chapter is major when its amount exceeds 400 MGL but does not exceed 900 MGL, very major when its amount exceeds 900 MGL.
2. A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 2, 4 or 6 of Article 281 of this Chapter shall be deemed intoxicated when intoxication of 0.41 or more per mille was established or when he avoided an intoxication check or consumed alcohol after a traffic accident until the establishment of its circumstances.
3. Road vehicles specified in Article 281 of this Chapter are motor vehicles, tractors, self-propelled machines and other mechanical vehicles, as well as autonomous automobiles.
Supplemented with an article:
No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published in TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

CHAPTER XL
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST PUBLIC ORDER

Article. Riots

1. A person who organised or provoked a crowd of people to publicly commit violence, destroy property or otherwise grossly violate public order, as well as a person who during riots committed violence, destroyed property or otherwise grossly violated public order,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2. A person who while performing the actions provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article used a firearm or explosives or resisted a police officer or other person performing public administration functions,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.

Article. Violation of public order

A person who in a public place by insolent conduct, threats, malicious mockery or vandalistic actions demonstrated disrespect to those around or to the environment and disturbed public peace or order,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-1871, 2015-06-25, published in TAR 2015-07-10,
i. k. 2015-11240

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version as of 2024-01-01

Article

False report of danger to the public or disaster
1.	A person who falsely reported or disseminated information about a danger threatening the public or a facility of state importance that meets the criteria established in the Civil Protection Law, or about a major disaster, if emergency services were called as a result,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or restriction of liberty, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2.	A person who falsely reported or disseminated information about a danger threatening the public or a facility of state importance that meets the criteria established in the Civil Protection Law, or about a major disaster, if this caused public panic or disrupted the operation of a facility of state importance,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
3.	A person who falsely reported or disseminated information about a danger threatening the public or a facility of state importance that meets the criteria established in the Civil Protection Law, or about a major disaster, if this caused major property damage,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)
Amendments to the Article:
No. XII-2170, 2015-12-15, published TAR 2015-12-21,
i. k. 2015-20140

CHAPTER XLI
CRIMES AGAINST THE ACTIVITIES OF A STATE OFFICIAL OR A PERSON PERFORMING PUBLIC ADMINISTRATION FUNCTIONS

Article

Resistance to a state official or a person performing public administration functions
A person who, by using or threatening to immediately use physical violence, resisted a state official or another person performing public administration functions,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Article

Threat to a state official or a person performing public administration functions
1.	A person who, by using mental coercion, demanded that a state official or a person performing public administration functions perform actions or refrain from them for the benefit of the offender or other persons,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A person who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article by using physical violence shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-2314, 2004-07-05, Žin., 2004, No. 108-4030 (2004-07-13)

Article

Interference with the activities of a state official or a person performing public administration functions
A state official, public figure, representative of a political or public organisation who, using his or her influence, interfered with the activities of a state official or a person performing public administration functions seeking to compel them to refrain from lawful actions or to perform unlawful actions for the benefit of himself or herself or other persons,

shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article

Usurpation of the name of a state official or a person performing public administration functions
A person who, having usurped the name of a state official or a person performing public administration functions, committed unlawful actions,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article. Repealed from 2016-04-01 Repeal of the Article

No. XII-1888, 2015-06-25, published TAR 2015-07-09,
i. k. 2015-11179

CHAPTER XLII
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE ORDER OF ADMINISTRATION

Article. Illegal crossing of the state border

1.	A person who illegally crossed the state border of the Republic of Lithuania shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A foreigner who illegally arrived in the Republic of Lithuania with the purpose of exercising the right to asylum shall be released from criminal liability under paragraph 1 of this Article.
3.	A foreigner who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article with the purpose of illegally crossing from the territory of the Republic of Lithuania into a third state shall be released from criminal liability under paragraph 1 of this Article if he or she is expelled in the prescribed manner to the state from whose territory he or she illegally crossed the state border of the Republic of Lithuania, or to the state of which he or she is a citizen.
Amendments to the Article:
No. IX-1495, 2003-04-10, Žin., 2003, No. 38-1733 (2003-04-24)

Article. Illegal transportation of persons across the state border

1.	A person who illegally transported across the state border of the Republic of Lithuania a foreigner who does not have a permanent place of residence in the Republic of Lithuania or transported or concealed in the territory of the Republic of Lithuania such a foreigner who illegally crossed the state border,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article for selfish motives, or if this endangered a person's life,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.
3.	A person who organised the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to ten years.
4.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-61, 2004-12-22, Žin., 2004, No. 188-6995 (2004-12-31)
Article 2921. Employment of third-country nationals illegally present in the Republic of Lithuania
1.	An employer or his or her authorised person who employed third-country nationals illegally present in the Republic of Lithuania for commercial purposes or employed five or more third-country nationals illegally present in the Republic of Lithuania, or employed a third-country national illegally present in the Republic of Lithuania under particularly exploitative working conditions, or employed a minor third-country national illegally present in the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an Article:
No. XI-1917, 2011-12-23, Žin., 2012, No. 4-115 (2012-01-06)

Article

Organisation of travel abroad by citizens of the Republic of Lithuania to remain there illegally or abandonment without assistance

1.	A person who organised citizens or permanent residents of the Republic of Lithuania to travel abroad to seek asylum or to work there illegally, or for other reasons to remain abroad illegally, or by fraudulently promising legal status abroad,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.
2.	A legal person shall also be liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Arbitrary conduct

1.	A person who, without complying with the procedure established by law, arbitrarily exercised a disputed or recognised but unrealised real or supposed right of his or her own or of another person and caused major damage to a person's rights or legitimate interests,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who committed arbitrary conduct by using mental or physical coercion against the victim or his or her
close person,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A person shall be liable for the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his or her legal representative, or a request by the prosecutor.

Article. Illegal installation or use of technical means

for collecting information
A person who illegally installed or used technical means for collecting information about a state or municipal institution, establishment or their official, political organisation or association or their member, other legal or natural person,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XIV-1384, 2022-07-19, published TAR 2022-07-20,
i. k. 2022-15855

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Seizure or Other Unlawful Acquisition of Official Secret

A person who seized, purchased or otherwise unlawfully acquired a material object whose content or information about it constitutes an official secret, or transferred such acquired object or information to a third person, where there were no elements of espionage or assistance to a foreign state to act against the Republic of Lithuania,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

Article. Disclosure of Official Secret

1.	A person who disclosed information constituting an official secret which was entrusted to him or which he learned due to his service or work, where there were no elements of espionage or assistance to a foreign state to act against the Republic of Lithuania, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty, or arrest.
2.	The act provided for in this Article shall also be criminal in cases where it was committed through
negligence.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1472, 2011-06-21, Žin., 2011, No. 81-3959 (2011-07-05)

Article. Repealed as of 2023-06-01 Repeal of Article

No. XIV-1925, 2023-04-27, published TAR 2023-05-04,
i. k. 2023-08475

Article. Failure to Render Assistance in Case of Collision of Vessels

A ship's captain who failed to render assistance in case of collision of vessels, where he could have rendered assistance without serious danger to his vessel, its crew and passengers,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

CHAPTER XLIII
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE ORDER OF ADMINISTRATION RELATED TO FORGERY OF DOCUMENTS OR MEASURING INSTRUMENTS

Article. Forgery of a Document or Disposal of a Forged Document

1.	A person who produced a false document, forged a genuine document or knowingly kept, transported, sent, used or disposed of a knowingly false
or knowingly forged genuine document, shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who produced a false identity card, passport, driving licence or state social insurance certificate or forged a genuine identity card, passport, driving licence or state social insurance certificate, or knowingly kept, transported, sent, used or disposed of a knowingly false or knowingly forged genuine identity card, passport, driving licence or state social insurance certificate,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to four years.
3.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraphs 1 or 2 of this Article, where major damage was caused as a result, or produced a large number of false identity cards, passports, driving licences or state social insurance certificates, or forged a large number of genuine identity cards, passports, driving licences or state social insurance certificates, or knowingly kept, transported, sent, used or disposed of a large number of knowingly false or knowingly forged genuine identity cards, passports, driving licences or state social insurance certificates,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
4.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-511, 2006-01-20, Žin., 2006, No. 17-605 (2006-02-11)

Article. Forgery of a Seal, Stamp or Form

1.	A person who forged a genuine or produced a false seal, stamp or strict accountability form of a natural or legal person or used a knowingly forged seal, stamp or form, or disposed of it,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article as a business or where these acts disrupted the activities of a natural or legal person, or caused major damage to the state or to a natural or legal person, or forged or kept a large number of false or forged seals, stamps or strict accountability forms,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Seizure of a Seal, Stamp or Document or Use of the Seized

1.	A person who seized or without lawful grounds acquired, kept, transported, sent, used or disposed of a seal, stamp, document or strict accountability form of a natural or legal person,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
2.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article as a business or seized or without lawful grounds acquired, kept, transported, sent, used or disposed of a large number of seals, stamps, documents or strict accountability forms of a natural or legal person, or where major damage was caused to the state or to a natural or legal person,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-511, 2006-01-20, Žin., 2006, No. 17-605 (2006-02-11)
302(1) Article. Production, Possession, Transportation, Sending or Disposal of Equipment for Forging Seals, Stamps, Documents or Strict Accountability Forms
1.	A person who produced, kept, transported, sent or disposed of equipment, software or other means directly intended or adapted for producing false seals, stamps, documents or strict accountability forms or for forging genuine seals, stamps, documents or strict accountability forms,

shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A person who committed the acts provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article as a business or where major damage was caused to the state or to a natural or legal person,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
The Code was supplemented with an article:
No. X-511, 2006-01-20, Žin., 2006, No. 17-605 (2006-02-11)

Article. Destruction, Damage or Concealment of a Seal, Stamp or Document

1.	A person who destroyed, damaged or concealed a seal, stamp, document or strict accountability form of a natural or legal person, where major damage was caused as a result,
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the acts provided for in this Article.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-2198, 2012-06-30, Žin., 2012, No. 82-4276 (2012-07-13)

Article. Provision of False Information in Order to Obtain a Document

1.	A person who, in order to obtain a document or confirmation of the authenticity of a forged document, provided false information to an institution or official, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine, or arrest.
2.	A legal person shall also be held liable for the act provided for in this Article.

Seizure, forgery, sale or use of a false impression of a state control hallmark or a control hallmark provided for in an international treaty of the Republic of Lithuania or of a foreign state
1. A person who seized, illegally processed, manufactured, used or sold an impression of a state control hallmark or a control hallmark provided for in an international treaty of the Republic of Lithuania or of a foreign state,
shall be punished by a fine or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for an act provided for in this Article.

CHAPTER XLIV
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST MORALITY

years.

Article. Profiting from another person's prostitution

1. A person who derived income from another person's prostitution or procured for prostitution,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to four

2. A person who organised or managed prostitution or transported a person with his or her consent

for prostitution to the Republic of Lithuania or from the Republic of Lithuania, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years.
3. A person who profited from the prostitution of a minor or organised or managed the prostitution of a minor or otherwise exploited a minor for the purposes of prostitution,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
4. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Involvement in prostitution

1. A person who involved a person in prostitution,
shall be punished by a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2. A person who involved in prostitution a person who is materially, by service or otherwise dependent or involved a person in prostitution by using physical or mental coercion or deception,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to seven years.
3. A person who recruited, forced to engage in prostitution or in any way involved a minor in prostitution,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.
4. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in this Article.

Article. Handling of items of pornographic content

1. A person who, with the purpose of distributing, manufactured or acquired or distributed items of pornographic content,
shall be punished by community service or a fine or restriction of liberty or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A person who manufactured, acquired, possessed, displayed, advertised, offered or distributed items of pornographic content depicting a child or a person presented as a child, or by using information and communication technologies or other means acquired or provided access to items of pornographic content depicting a child or a person presented as a child,
shall be punished by a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to four years.

3. A person who, with the purpose of distributing, manufactured or acquired or distributed a large quantity of items of pornographic content depicting an infant child,

shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
4. A person who displayed or advertised items of pornographic content shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest.
5. A legal person shall also be held liable for acts provided for in parts 1, 2 and 3 of this Article.

Article. Cruel treatment of animals

1. A person who treated an animal cruelly, tortured it, if as a result the animal died or was maimed,
shall be punished by community service or a fine or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to one
year.
2. A legal person shall also be held liable for an act provided for in this Article.

CHAPTER XLV
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST THE MEMORY OF THE DECEASED

Article. Desecration of the remains of the deceased

1. A person who illegally took the remains of the deceased or a part thereof or mocked the remains of the deceased or desecrated them,
shall be punished by community service or restriction of liberty or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2. A person who illegally exhumed a grave and desecrated the remains of the deceased or mocked them or took
items that were there,
shall be punished by arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Article. Desecration of a grave or other place of public respect

1. A person who destroyed or otherwise desecrated a grave or damaged a monument or desecrated another place of public respect,
shall be punished by community service or restriction of liberty or arrest or imprisonment for a term of up to one year.
2. A person who committed acts of vandalism in a cemetery or other place of public respect or
for racial, national or religious motives desecrated a grave or other place of public respect, shall be punished by community service or a fine or imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. X-1233, 2007-06-28, Žin., 2007, No. 81-3309 (2007-07-21)

Article. Disparagement of the memory of the deceased

1. A person who, seeking to disparage the deceased or his or her relatives, disrupted the solemnity of a funeral shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest.
2. A person who publicly disseminated false fabrications about the deceased that could cause people's contempt or undermine respect for his or her memory shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by community service or a fine or restriction of liberty or arrest.
3. A person shall be held liable for acts provided for in parts 1 and 2 of this Article only if there is a complaint by the victim or a statement by his or her legal representative or a request by the prosecutor.
CHAPTER XLVI
CRIMES AND CRIMINAL MISDEMEANOURS AGAINST NATIONAL DEFENCE SERVICE AND MOBILISATION
The title of the chapter has been amended:
No. XIII-3231, 2020-06-30, published in TAR 2020-07-16,
i. k. 2020-15865

Article. Evasion of conscription into compulsory military service

1.	A person liable for military service who evaded conscription into compulsory military service by impairing his health, simulating illness or health disorder, forging documents or by other fraud,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A person liable for military service who evaded conscription into compulsory military service, where there were no elements specified in paragraph 1 of this Article, shall have committed a criminal misdemeanour and
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1520, 2011-06-23, Žin., 2011, No. 86-4162 (2011-07-13)

Article. Evasion of conscription into military service during martial law

A person liable for military service who evaded conscription into military service during martial law shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1520, 2011-06-23, Žin., 2011, No. 86-4162 (2011-07-13)

Article. Evasion of military service

1.	A serviceman who evaded performing active military service by impairing his health or by simulating illness, forging documents or by other fraud,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
2.	A serviceman who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article during martial law or while performing a combat mission, or where serious consequences resulted from that act,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.
Article 3161. Failure to perform essential functions during mobilisation or martial law
A person who, being entered in the reserve of civilian mobilisation personnel, evaded performing the functions assigned to him during mobilisation or martial law or performed them improperly, where serious consequences resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by a fine or by arrest, or by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
Supplemented by Article:
No. XIII-3231, 2020-06-30, published TAR 2020-07-16,
i. k. 2020-15865

Article. Failure to obey an order

1.	A serviceman who failed to obey a commander's order or refused to obey it or otherwise disobeyed a commander's order,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A serviceman who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article during martial law or while performing a combat mission, or where serious consequences resulted from that act,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to six years.
3.	A serviceman who failed to obey a manifestly unlawful order of a commander shall not be held criminally liable.

Article. Threat to a commander or violence against him

1.	A serviceman who threatened a commander with violence in connection with his military service duties shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A serviceman who used physical violence against a commander in connection with his military service duties shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.
3.	A serviceman who committed the act provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article during martial law or while performing a combat mission, or where serious consequences resulted from that act,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.

Article. Acts of violence against a subordinate

1.	A commander who, in the performance of military service duties, used physical violence against a subordinate serviceman shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A commander who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article, where serious consequences resulted therefrom,

shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to eight years.

Article. Terrorising a serviceman

1.	A serviceman who unlawfully demanded that another serviceman act according to his instruction, or who humiliated another serviceman by using psychological coercion,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A serviceman who humiliated or terrorised another serviceman by using physical violence or a weapon shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
3.	A serviceman who committed the act specified in paragraphs 1 and 2 of this Article, where serious consequences resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.

Article. Unlawful order and its execution

A serviceman who gave a manifestly unlawful order or forced another serviceman to execute such an order, as well as a serviceman who executed a manifestly unlawful order, where serious consequences resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to eight years.

Article. Unauthorised absence

1.	A serviceman who was absent without leave from his unit or place of service or failed to arrive at his unit or place of service at the appointed time without justifiable reasons, where the absence lasted more than three days but not more than ten days,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.
2.	A serviceman who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article during martial law or while performing a combat mission,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to eight years.

Article. Desertion

1.	A serviceman who absented himself from his unit or place of service for a period exceeding ten days or failed to arrive at his unit or place of service for such period, or who, regardless of the duration of absence, did so with the intention of evading military service,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A serviceman who committed the act provided for in paragraph 1 of this Article during martial law or while performing a combat mission,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of four to eight years.

Article. Loss of national defence property

A serviceman who, through negligence, lost a weapon, ammunition, explosives, explosive substances, a vehicle or other military equipment entrusted to him for service purposes,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

Article. Violation of guard duty regulations

A serviceman who violated the requirements of legal acts regulating guard duty during martial law or while performing a combat mission, or where serious consequences resulted from that act,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Article. Violation of state border protection service regulations

1.	A serviceman who, during martial law while performing the state border protection service of the Republic of Lithuania, violated the regulations of this service, where serious consequences resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.
2.	A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through
negligence.

Article. Violation of duty service regulations

1.	A serviceman who violated the requirements of legal acts regulating duty service while performing duty service in a unit (except guard duty), where serious consequences resulted therefrom,
shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years.

2.	A person shall be held liable under this Article only in cases where the acts provided for therein were committed through
negligence.

Article. Abandonment of a sinking warship

The commander of a warship who abandoned a sinking warship without fulfilling all his duties shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years.

Article. Loss or desecration of a military unit's flag

A serviceman who lost a unit's flag or desecrated it,
shall be punished by arrest or by imprisonment for a term of up to two years.

This excerpt sits under: Consolidated version from 2024-01-01

Article. Interpretation of Concepts

1. Soldier – a citizen of the Republic of Lithuania performing actual military service, and in time of war – a person who is a member of the armed forces. Amendments to the part of the Article:
No. XIII-3231, 2020-06-30, published TAR 2020-07-16,
i. d. 2020-15865

2. Person liable for military service – an adult citizen of the Republic of Lithuania who has a military obligation. Amendments to the Article:
No. XI-1520, 2011-06-23, Žin., 2011, No. 86-4162 (2011-07-13)

31. Seimas of the Republic of Lithuania, Law
No. XI-1199, 2010-12-02, Žin., 2010, No. 145-7439 (2010-12-11)
LAW AMENDING AND SUPPLEMENTING ARTICLES 3, 67, 72, 190 OF THE CRIMINAL CODE AND

The extended confiscation of property provided for in Article 723 of the Criminal Code set out in Article 4 of this law shall apply only to property acquired by a person who has committed an act prohibited by criminal law after the entry into force of this law. Pursuant to Article 1891 of the Criminal Code set out in Article 5 of this law, only persons who possess property meeting the characteristics specified in Article 1891 of the Criminal Code after the entry into force of this law shall be held criminally liable. 32.
